Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [235] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个被忽视的安全漏洞：大语言模型在训练后量化（post-training quantization）阶段可能被植入后门攻击。作者提出了"量化行为等价类"（QBECs）理论框架，证明全精度源检查通过并不能保证量化部署后的行为等价性，并通过三阶段对抗微调在INT8和4-bit压缩激活后门。实验表明该攻击在机器翻译和政治文本分析场景中有效，攻击在不同量化方案和架构间具有可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化常被视为边缘部署的语义中立优化过程。当模型在源精度（全精度）下评估通过后再进行量化部署，缺乏等效的重新评估，形成了结构性的"验证-部署鸿沟"。由于量化是参数空间上的多对一映射，源精度的认证并不能保证部署配置中的行为等价性，这为量化触发的后门攻击提供了理论空间，本文旨在形式化并实证这一威胁。

Method: 1) 形式化定义量化行为等价类（QBEC），并证明QBEC成员关系不蕴含行为等价性。2) 提出三阶段对抗微调框架，在全精度模型中嵌入潜在恶意载荷，使其通过源精度检查但在INT8或4-bit压缩时激活攻击行为。3) 将攻击从decoder-only因果语言模型扩展到多语言encoder-decoder序列到序列模型，并评估两个操作场景：战术机器翻译与政治内容分析。4) 进行跨量化器迁移性分析。

Result: 后门攻击的翻译模型在FP16修复状态下朋友-敌人损坏率为零，量化后反转率达到85.02%；配对立场分类器在压缩后意识形态偏移ΔBias=0.33。跨量化器分析表明攻击持续性与量化方案和模型架构相关，而非仅取决于标称位宽。

Conclusion: 源精度审计本身无法排除量化触发行为，最终部署配置必须纳入行为认证范围，以确保可信的边缘AI部署安全。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [236] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAMP是首个针对Gated DeltaNet (GDN)和Kimi Delta Attention (KDA)等线性注意力模型中循环状态进行训练后量化的方法。通过发现量化误差能量集中在少数通道、且状态通道的相对衰减强度在不同提示和任务间保持稳定这一观察，DAMP在离线校准阶段识别高风险通道并分配更高精度，其余通道使用INT8，在保持精度的同时显著减少推理内存和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GDN和KDA的语言模型用固定大小的循环状态替代KV缓存以降低推理内存，但这些循环状态通常以FP32存储，占用大量GPU显存，其更新是内存带宽瓶颈，显著增加解码延迟。此前尚无针对此类循环状态进行训练后量化的研究，而简单的均匀量化（INT8/FP8精度）在复杂推理任务上会导致精度下降，更低精度（INT4/NVFP4）则几乎完全失效。

Method: DAMP首先观察到两点关键发现：(1) 大多数量化误差能量集中在少量通道中；(2) 状态通道的相对衰减强度在不同提示和任务间保持稳定。基于这些发现，DAMP在离线校准阶段综合量化误差能量和基于衰减的持久性两个指标来识别高风险（高精度需求）通道，对这些通道以更高精度存储，其余通道使用INT8量化，实现混合精度存储方案。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上跨越数学推理、一般推理和代码生成六个基准进行评估。在平均9.9比特每状态值的条件下，DAMP保持平均精度接近FP32基线；将循环状态存储减少了69.1%；将循环状态更新内核加速至多2.01倍；将全模型TPOT（每个输出token的延迟）降低至多10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP证明了通过混合精度量化（基于误差能量和衰减持久性的通道感知策略）可以有效压缩GDN/KDA模型中的循环状态，在不牺牲推理精度的前提下大幅降低存储开销和提升解码速度，为该类线性注意力架构语言模型的推理优化提供了可行的量化方案。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [237] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新颖的块稀疏特征化器（BSF），它是稀疏自编码器（SAE）的变体，以子空间（方向块）而非单个方向为原子单元，专门针对视觉中常见的低维流形特征设计。作者分析了BSF的优缺点，发现它仍存在经典SAE的失败模式（特征分裂和组合），提出了包括Tournament Top-K选择规则在内的多项架构改进以显著减少特征分裂，并将块范式扩展到了跨编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SAE以单个方向为原子单元，但对于视觉中广泛存在的低维流形特征（如物体姿态、光照变化等连续变化的特征），单个方向难以充分表征。BSF通过使用块（子空间）作为原子单元试图解决这一问题，但其表现仍需系统评估和改进。

Method: 1）系统评估BSF在视觉特征上的表现，识别其仍然存在的经典SAE失败模式（特征分裂和组合）；2）提出多种架构改进，其中核心创新是Tournament Top-K选择规则，通过锦标赛式的特征选择机制来降低特征分裂；3）将块范式从单编码器扩展至crosscoder架构，使其能处理更广泛的特征分析任务。

Result: Tournament Top-K选择规则显著减少了BSF中的特征分裂现象，且块范式成功拓展到了crosscoder架构中，表明该改进方案在减轻经典SAE失败模式方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: BSF虽为视觉低维流形特征设计，但仍不免受经典SAE失败模式影响；通过引入Tournament Top-K选择规则和扩展块范式至crosscoder，可以有效改善特征分裂问题，为后续视觉特征的稀疏分解研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [238] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了持续学习（CL）和模型合并（MM）中的灾难性遗忘与权重解缠错误本质上是同一现象——任务干扰，并将其形式化为层间Frobenius内积。作者从理论上证明优化器通过控制谱范数‖ΔW_ℓ‖₂来调节任务干扰，并发现Muon优化器天然具备此调节能力，在CL和MM基准上均优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将持续学习和模型合并中的困难视为独立问题并分别解决，优化器的几何特性被视为实现细节。作者旨在揭示这两个挑战的统一本质，并探索优化器在其中的关键作用。

Method: 提出任务干扰（task interference）这一统一框架，将其形式化为层间Frobenius内积⟨ΔW_ℓ, J_ℓ(x)⟩_F；理论上推导出隔离谱范数‖ΔW_ℓ‖₂作为可被优化器控制的任务干扰上界；通过逐模式分析证明该界能追踪经验干扰的支配部分；识别Muon优化器在构造上天然调节该因子。

Result: 用Muon替代AdamW在八任务模型合并基准上（三种CLIP骨干）准确率最高提升+5.02点；在持续学习方面，Muon在十个类增量协议、三个任务增量协议及11任务MTIL基准上均取得一致的正向收益。

Conclusion: 任务干扰是CL和MM困难的统一根源，谱范数控制是优化器调节任务干扰的关键机制。Muon作为一种以优化器为中心的、与现有方法互补的原则性方案，通过收紧干扰上界在两类任务中均带来显著提升。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error. In the literature, these difficulties are merely treated separately and mitigated through a variety of solutions, while the geometry induced by the base optimizer is treated as an implementation detail. In this work, we show that the two difficulties are in fact two instances of the same phenomenon: a parameter update useful for one task shifts the model's outputs on another. We formalize this shared phenomenon as \textit{task interference} and reduce it to a common layer-wise Frobenius inner product $\langle ΔW_\ell, J_\ell(x)\rangle_F$. This quantity, in turn, is utilized to expose the role of the optimizer. We theoretically derive an upper bound that isolates the spectral norm $\|ΔW_\ell\|_2$ as an optimizer-controllable factor of task interference, and a per-mode analysis shows that this bound tracks the dominant part of the empirical interference. Specifically, we then identify the recent Muon optimizer as a mechanism that regulates this factor by construction. Our work reveals that its elegant control on spectral norm tightens the interference bound for both CL and MM, positioning Muon as a principled optimizer-centric approach complementary to existing solutions. Our theoretcal analysis is well validated by experimental results. Replacing the AdamW optimizer with Muon improves accuracy by up to +5.02 points on the eight-task model-merging benchmark across three CLIP backbones. For continual learning, Muon also delivers uniformly positive gains across ten class-incremental protocols, three task-incremental protocols, and the 11-task MTIL benchmark.

</details>


### [239] [Dandelion: A Spherical Flower for Neural Simulation of Planetary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27521)
*Till Muser,Giovanni Abati,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dandelion，一种球面版本的神经偏微分方程求解器，通过预测切平面位移并沿大圆传输特征来实现空间混合，避免了传统卷积操作，并在新的球面PDE基准套件上取得了最佳或次佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力学过程在球面上展开，但现有的科学机器学习架构默认基于欧几里得空间。直接将这些架构应用于规则经纬网格会导致问题：笛卡尔卷积在高纬度变形、傅里叶神经算子中的2D FFT错误假设双重周期性、ViT中的笛卡尔位置编码扭曲球面测地距离。此外，现有球面数据集要么过小过于理想化，要么过大（如ERA5）不利于模型迭代。

Method: Dandelion是Flower（基于warp的神经PDE求解器）的球面扩展版本。其每一层预测切平面位移，并沿大圆（great circles）传输特征。通过完全在球谐域中实现分层池化来构建U-Net风格结构，不使用任何卷积，仅通过球面坐标变换（warp）实现空间混合。同时发布了一个包含多种挑战性球面PDE数据集（包括改进的Galewsky急流、异常链式湍流、Cahn-Hilliard分解、球面Riemann激波、Held-Suarez干大气输运和全球海洋动力学）的基准套件。

Result: Dandelion在所有数据集上均取得最佳或次佳性能，且与非warp基线的差距随分辨率增大而扩大。在256×512分辨率下，Dandelion和Flower2D在单步预测和滚动预测中均占据前两名。

Conclusion: 基于warp的方法（如Dandelion）为球面PDE求解提供了优于传统卷积或谱方法的替代方案，且其优势在高分辨率下更为显著。文中发布的基准套件填补了现有球面数据集的空白，为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Many dynamical processes unfold on the sphere but the default scientific machine learning architectures are Euclidean. Applying these architectures on a regular lat-lon grid causes problems: Cartesian convolutions become distorted at high latitude; 2D FFTs in Fourier neural operators incorrectly assume double periodicity; Cartesian positional encodings in ViTs distort spherical geodesic distances. Recent work moves towards natively spherical primitives, including spherical convolutions (e.g., DeepSphere or DISCO), Spherical Fourier Neural Operators (SFNOs), and geodesic attention. Here we propose Dandelion, a spherical version of Flower, a warp-based neural PDE solver. Layers of Dandelion predict a tangent-plane displacement and transport features along great circles. We obtain a U-Net-like structure by implementing hierarchical pooling entirely in the spherical-harmonic domain. There are thus no convolutions: spatial mixing is achieved only through spherical coordinate changes, or warps. To compare Dandelion with existing spherical architectures, we release an evolving benchmark suite of challenging, natively-spherical PDE datasets including a modified Galewsky jet, anomalous chained turbulence, Cahn-Hilliard decomposition, spherical Riemann shocks, Held-Suarez dry atmospheric transport and global ocean dynamics. This new benchmark fills the gap in existing spherical datasets which are either too small and stylized, or much too large (ERA5) for model iteration. Dandelion is best or second-best on every dataset, and the gap to non-warp baselines widens with resolution: at $256\times 512$, Dandelion and Flower2D occupy the top two slots in both single-step prediction and rollout.

</details>


### [240] [Self-Explainable Multi-Label Graph Neural Network for Correlated Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27574)
*Yingqi Feng,Yufei Tang,Min Shi,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEMGNN（自解释多标签图神经网络），它是首个在训练阶段集成可解释能力的多标签图学习方法，通过联合学习预测器与稀疏边掩码解释器，并利用标签间相关性增强分类与解释，实现多标签节点分类与标签条件化解释的同步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多标签图学习方法缺乏训练时可解释能力；事后（post-hoc）图解释器虽已存在，但未在多标签图学习器中显式建模标签依赖的证据共享，尤其当标签对弱相关或负相关时，事后方法无法正确判断跨标签的证据应共享还是分离。

Method: SEMGNN采用端到端统一框架，联合学习一个预测器和一个稀疏边掩码解释器。通过标签-标签相关性约束，在提升多标签节点分类性能的同时增强单标签解释质量，使同一节点的不同标签可由不同但连贯的结构性/相关性证据支持。

Result: 在社交网络、娱乐和生命科学领域的合成与真实多标签网络上，SEMGNN在预测性能上达到竞争性甚至更优水平，同时提供更忠实且更紧凑的标签条件化解释。

Conclusion: SEMGNN验证了将可解释性内置于多标签图学习训练过程的可行性，相比事后解释方法能更好地处理标签间弱相关/负相关情形下的证据共享与分离问题，兼具预测性能与解释质量。

Abstract: Multi-label graph learning intends to capture the intrinsic complexity of real-world applications, where one sample is often related to multiple groups or consists of multiple objects. To date, a handful of multi-label graph learning methods exist, but none of them integrate training-time interpretation capability. While post-hoc graph explainers have been developed, they do not explicitly model label-dependent evidence sharing in multi-label graph learners, especially when label pairs are weakly or negatively associated. As a result, post-hoc approaches may miss how evidence should be shared or separated across different labels. This paper advances a new end-to-end self-explainable multi-label graph neural network (SEMGNN), which aims to simultaneously classify multi-labeled nodes and identify edges significantly contributing to each target node w.r.t. predicted labels. Different from post-hoc methods, SEMGNN jointly learns a predictor and a sparse edge-mask explainer within a unified framework and training objective. Label-label correlations are used to improve multi-label node classification and enhance individual label explanations, so that different labels of a node can be supported by distinct yet coherent structural and/or correlated evidence. Experiments and comparisons on synthetic and real-world multi-label networks, in social networking, entertainment, and life sciences, show that SEMGNN achieves competitive or improved predictive performance while providing more faithful and compact label-conditioned explanations.

</details>


### [241] [Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27634)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARSANN是一个基于局部曲率的自适应最近邻分类框架，通过形状算子估计局部平均曲率来控制邻域空间范围：高曲率区域邻域半径收缩，平坦区域保持较宽支持。在70多个OpenML数据集上超越了标准k-NN，并在45个数据集的受控比较中40个取得更高平衡精度（均值从0.6506提升至0.7528），统计检验确认提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-NN在整个特征空间中施加统一的邻域基数（k值），但现实数据的内在流形几何结构在不同区域差异显著，统一的邻域大小无法适应局部几何复杂度的变化。现有自适应方法只调整邻域数量或局部度量，未显式调节空间支持范围。

Method: 1) 使用TwoNN估计内在维度，并通过PCA构建内在表示；2) 基于形状算子（shape-operator）公式估计局部平均曲率；3) 以曲率控制邻域尺度：高曲率区域施加更强的半径收缩，平坦区域保留更宽的空间支持；4) 显式调整邻域的空间范围而非仅调整邻居数量或局部度量。

Result: 在70多个真实OpenML数据集上，CARSANN一致优于标准k-NN且与自适应最近邻方法具有竞争力。在相同基邻域大小的受控比较中，45个数据集中的40个获得更高平衡精度，平均平衡精度从0.6506提升至0.7528。相对于固定k=5的k-NN优势依然保持，Friedman和Nemenyi检验确认改进具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 局部流形曲率可以作为有效的几何控制变量来适应邻域支持范围，为基于基数的最近邻自适应方法提供了一种互补的范式。曲率驱动的空间调整比单纯的邻居数量调整更能捕捉数据局部几何结构的变化。

Abstract: Nearest neighbor classification relies fundamentally on how locality is defined, yet conventional $k$-NN imposes the same neighborhood cardinality throughout the feature space. This assumption can be inadequate for data whose local geometry varies substantially across the underlying manifold. We introduce Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification (CARSANN), a geometry-driven framework that adapts the spatial support of each neighborhood according to local geometric complexity. CARSANN first estimates intrinsic dimensionality using TwoNN and constructs an intrinsic representation through principal component analysis. Local mean curvature is then estimated using a shape-operator-based formulation and controls neighborhood scale: highly curved regions receive stronger radius shrinkage, whereas approximately flat regions retain broader spatial support. Unlike methods that modify only the number of neighbors or the local metric, CARSANN explicitly adapts the spatial extent of local evidence. Experiments on more than 70 real-world OpenML datasets show that CARSANN consistently improves upon standard $k$-NN and is competitive with adaptive nearest-neighbor methods. In a controlled comparison using the same base neighborhood size, CARSANN achieves higher balanced accuracy on 40 of 45 datasets, increasing mean balanced accuracy from 0.6506 to 0.7528. The advantage also persists against $k$-NN with fixed $k=5$. Friedman and Nemenyi tests confirm that the improvements are statistically significant. These results indicate that local manifold curvature can serve as an effective geometric control variable for adapting neighborhood support, providing a complementary paradigm to cardinality-based nearest-neighbor adaptation.

</details>


### [242] [More Data Cannot Break a Symmetry: Identifiability by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27651)
*Jing Xu,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于廉价的非平凡重标记（最便宜的非恒等重标记）作为设计阶段诊断工具的框架，用于检测无监督表征对齐中的退化问题。研究发现，密集采样产生的近重复项会导致传统诊断方法失效，并通过色彩实验证明该失败是结构性的。最终，仅通过该诊断选择刺激（不咨询任何学习到的表征），即可将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表征对齐旨在仅从几何结构恢复刺激间的一一对应关系，但刺激几何的自同构群从根本上限制了任何对齐方法能识别的内容。研究者需要一个在设计阶段就能检测这种退化性的诊断工具，而现有的最廉价非恒等重标记诊断方法在密集采样下排序错误，因此需要更可靠的诊断方法。

Method: 作者将已知的不变性（Demetci et al., 2024）转化为设计阶段的诊断工具和干预措施。在具有闭式几何的色彩领域，通过比较对称设计与非对称设计在极大重启预算下的恢复表现，证明退化是结构性的。使用该诊断（而非任何学习表征）来选择九种颜色，并评估所有93个模型表征的表现。

Result: 仅通过诊断选择九种颜色，无需咨询任何学习到的表征，即可使所有93个模型表征远离退化点，将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%（模型、层、N和求解器均保持固定）。此外，区分表征模型与恢复对应关系本质上是不相关的目标（r = -0.02，超过3000个子集）。

Conclusion: 同样的退化风险存在于任何规则设计遇到其候选几何等距群的场景中，包括均匀间隔的方向、音调或运动方向。该检查在数据收集前仅需一次函数调用即可完成，是一种成本极低且有效的设计阶段干预手段。

Abstract: Unsupervised representational alignment recovers a stimulus-by-stimulus correspondence from geometry alone, but the automorphism group of the stimulus geometry bounds what any such alignment can identify, before data exist. The obvious diagnostic for this degeneracy, the cheapest non-identity relabelling, ranks two published designs in the wrong order, because dense sampling creates near-duplicates whose transposition is nearly free. We turn this known invariance (Demetci et al., 2024) into a design-time diagnostic and intervention. In colour, where candidate geometries have closed form, we show that the failure is structural: sixty-four times the restart budget leaves a symmetric design unmoved while an asymmetric set at the same N recovers every time. Discriminating representational models and recovering a correspondence are essentially uncorrelated objectives (r = -0.02 over 3,000 subsets). Choosing nine colours by this diagnostic alone, without consulting any learned representation, moves all 93 model representations away from the degenerate point and cuts catastrophic alignment failures from 75% to 2% with the models, the layers, N and the solver all held fixed. The same risk arises wherever a regular design meets its candidate geometry's isometry group, including evenly spaced orientations, tones, or motion directions, and the check costs one function call before data collection.

</details>


### [243] [Unsupervised Continual Learning with Growing Self-Organizing Maps and Synthetic Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27662)
*Pujan Thapa,Alexander Ororbia,Travis Desell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于增长自组织映射（GSOM）的生成式持续学习框架，通过为每个GSOM单元学习分布统计量（均值、方差、协方差），实现无需存储原始数据的无样例回放，并可在编码器-解码器配置下通过祖先采样生成合成样本进行训练。该方法完全无监督，不依赖任务边界或类别标签，在多个基准上达到与监督式最先进方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习方法通常需要存储原始样本（记忆重放）或依赖任务边界与类标签（监督式），这限制了其在隐私敏感、存储受限场景下的适用性。作者旨在探索一种无需样例存储、无监督自适应的持续学习机制，以解决灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 该方法基于增长自组织映射（GSOM）。每个GSOM单元维护自身的均值、方差和协方差估计，用于生成合成样本作为回放数据（分布统计记忆）。在编码器-解码器配置中，合成样本通过祖先采样解码回输入空间用于后续训练。整个训练过程完全无监督，不依赖显式任务边界或类别标签。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到了与监督式最先进的基于记忆的方法相竞争的性能，并一致优于无记忆方法。在具有挑战性的单类增量学习场景中，该方法在若干设置下匹配或超越了现有基线。此外，作者提供了单类增量TinyImageNet和MiniImageNet的基线结果。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了一种无监督、自适应的、由拓扑驱动的神经统计回放方法作为持续学习中可扩展且灵活方案的有效性，为无需原始数据存储和无监督持续学习提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: This work presents a generative continual learning framework based on growing self-organizing maps (GSOMs) that are augmented with learned distributional statistics as well as encoder-decoder models for class-incremental learning. The proposed approach enables exemplar-free replay using distributional statistical memory, which eliminates the need to store raw data. Each GSOM unit maintains its own mean, variance, and covariance estimates, which are subsequently used to generate synthetic samples for replay; in encoder-decoder configurations, these samples are then decoded back into the input space (via ancestral sampling) for subsequent training. Our method is fully unsupervised, as it does not rely on explicit task boundaries or class labels during training. Results across multiple benchmarks show that the proposed approach achieves performance competitive even with supervised state-of-the-art memory-based methods while consistently outperforming memory-free approaches. In several settings, our framework matches or exceeds existing baselines, particularly in challenging single-class incremental scenarios. We also provide baseline results for single-class incremental TinyImageNet and MiniImageNet, offering a useful reference for future work. This work highlights the effectiveness of an unsupervised, adaptive, topology-driven neural form of statistical replay as a scalable, flexible approach to continual learning.

</details>


### [244] [SegBench-GC: Testing Segmentation Invariance in Multi-Step Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27678)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SegBench-GC，一个受控的离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）分段不变性压力测试基准。实验表明，日志轨迹中仅因管理原因（而非真实终止）产生的人工分段边界，会显著影响多步离线GCRL算法的性能：在PointMaze中，未分段成功率50.5%，采用延续有效目标（CVT）为39.1%，而朴素处理仅19.1%；独立发表的n-step基线在Puzzle-4x5上同样显示出严重失败（朴素处理仅0.27%成功率）。核心贡献在于提供受控基准、故障隔离以及跨学习器的证据，证明管理性分段会实质性地改变多步离线GCRL结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习经常利用轨迹结构进行未来目标采样和多步目标计算，但记录日志时轨迹可能因行政管理原因被切分，这些切分边界与真实终止无关。现有方法未系统评估这种"虚假分段"对算法的影响，因此需要构建受控基准来隔离并量化该问题。

Method: 作者引入SegBench-GC基准，控制转移、源轨迹、目标采样、优化设置和评估均保持不变，仅改变人工备份边界的位置以及这些边界是否保留延续值。延续有效目标（CVT）作为分段一致性对照组：奖励累积在人工切分处停止，但目标从存储的后继状态（stored successor）引导。实验在PointMaze中进行匹配数量研究（35,000个人工切分、3种分段实现、3个优化种子），并在Puzzle-4x5上使用独立发表的n-step基线（n=25）进行验证。

Result: PointMaze实验中，最终50-episode-per-task成功率：未分段为50.5%，CVT为39.1%，朴素处理仅19.1%；不同分段实现下朴素平均成功率范围在4.8%到31.9%之间。独立的n=25基线在Puzzle-4x5上：未分段47.2%，CVT 58.5%，朴素处理仅0.27%。目标级诊断验证了分析目标差异达到数值精度，学习评论家诊断显示朴素处理产生大幅乐观偏移，而CVT与未分段评论家保持近似对齐。

Conclusion: 管理性分段（非真实终止）会实质性改变多步离线GCRL的性能，朴素处理将人工切分视作吸收态会导致严重性能退化。CVT采用标准的延续引导（continuation bootstrapping）而非新的Bellman规则即可大幅缓解此问题。本文的贡献在于受控基准、故障隔离机制以及跨学习器证据，而非提出新的算法规则。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) often uses trajectory structure for future-goal sampling and multi-step targets, yet logged trajectories may be partitioned for administrative reasons that do not correspond to termination. We introduce SegBench-GC, a controlled stress test of segmentation invariance that holds transitions, source trajectories, goal sampling, optimization settings, and evaluation fixed while varying only artificial backup boundaries and whether those boundaries retain continuation value. Continuation-valid targets (CVT) provide the segmentation-consistent control: reward accumulation stops at an artificial cut, but the target bootstraps from its stored successor. In a matched-count PointMaze study with 35,000 artificial cuts, three segmentation realizations, and three optimization seeds, final 50-episode-per-task success is 50.5% uncut, 39.1% with CVT, and 19.1% when the same cuts are treated as absorbing; across segmentation realizations, naive mean success ranges from 4.8% to 31.9%. An independent published n-step baseline (n=25) from the Decoupled Q-Chunking codebase shows the same failure on Puzzle-4x5: 47.2% uncut, 58.5% CVT, and 0.27% naive across three optimization seeds. A target-level diagnostic verifies the analytic target difference to numerical precision, and learned-critic diagnostics show a large optimistic shift under naive handling while CVT remains approximately aligned with the uncut critic. CVT applies standard continuation bootstrapping rather than a new Bellman rule; the contribution is the controlled benchmark, failure isolation, and cross-learner evidence that administrative segmentation can materially change multi-step offline GCRL.

</details>


### [245] [SafeStep: An Interactive Demonstration of Semantic Communication for Pedestrian Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27688)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Jeremiah Yang,Sirin Chakraborty,Samuel Chamoun,Travis Ross,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeStep是一个基于浏览器的交互式语义通信平台，用于实时行人安全监控。该平台从四个实时交通摄像头提取行人信息，通过语义通信收发器在AWGN信道上传输，并在浏览器中渲染用户特定的位置、轨迹和风险标签。平台支持独立选择收发器、SNR、码长和AoI参数，并首次使用户能够直接观察到AoI-induced下游降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信的理论研究日益增多，但缺乏一个实时的、用户可交互的语义通信平台来直观展示不同收发器和参数配置在实际应用中的性能表现，特别是在行人安全监控这类对实时性和可靠性要求较高的场景中。

Method: SafeStep平台集成四种实时交通摄像头数据源，利用语义通信收发器（包括Meta-VIB和五个基线收发器）在AWGN信道上传输行人信息。平台允许用户独立选择收发器类型、SNR、码长和AoI参数，并在每个用户浏览器上实时展示所选配置下的行人监控效果。Meta-VIB采用仅416万参数的紧凑神经模型，可在不同SNR、码长和AoI条件下泛化而无需在线重训练。

Result: Meta-VIB在任务损失上平均降低高达92.1%。在高性能GPU服务器上，集成的并发访问工作负载在20个用户时维持了5帧/秒的目标帧率；在100个用户、每个用户请求不同配置时，无请求失败且平均应用响应时间低于1秒，但每浏览器平均帧率降至约1帧/秒。SafeStep是首个使AoI-induced下游降级在实时监控应用中可直接观察的实时语义通信平台。

Conclusion: SafeStep展示了语义通信在实时行人安全监控中的可行性，证明了Meta-VIB在不同配置下的优越性能，并通过该平台使用户能够直观观察到语义通信参数（尤其是AoI）对监控质量的实时影响，为语义通信的实践应用和性能验证提供了有价值的平台基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop SafeStep, an interactive browser-based semantic communication platform for live pedestrian safety monitoring. SafeStep extracts pedestrian information from four live traffic-camera feeds, transmits it through a semantic communication transceiver over an Additive White Gaussian Noise (AWGN) channel, and renders user-specific positions, trajectories, and risk labels. The platform allows to independently select the transceiver, Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR), codelength, and Age of Information (AoI), and demonstrates the transceiver performance of the selected configuration through live pedestrian safety monitoring to each browser. SafeStep compares a recently proposed semantic communication design called Meta-VIB with five baseline transceivers. Meta-VIB uses a compact neural model with only $4.16$ million parameters to generalize across varying SNR, codelength, and AoI values without online retraining. Experimental results show that Meta-VIB achieves mean task-loss reductions of up to $92.1\%$. On one high-end GPU server, the integrated concurrent-access workload maintains the target $5$ frames/s through $20$ users. At $100$ users, each requesting a distinct configuration, SafeStep records no request failures and a mean application response time below $1$ s, but its mean per-browser frame rate falls to approximately $1$ frame/s. To our knowledge, SafeStep is the first real-time semantic communication platform to make AoI-induced downstream degradation directly observable in live monitoring applications.

</details>


### [246] [RiskBlend: A Multi-Signal Framework for Test Input Prioritization in Machine Learning Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27704)
*Madhusudan Srinivasan,Namith Nishal Raphae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiskBlend框架，结合历史失败模式、预测偏移、决策边界偏移和邻域变化四种互补风险信号，用于机器学习分类器重训练后的回归缺陷测试输入优先级排序。在1200个实验配置中，RiskBlend在所有80个数据集-分类器-场景组合中取得最高平均APFD，较最强基线提升最高0.32。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习分类器重训练后，先前正确分类的输入可能被新版本误分类，产生回归缺陷。检测这些缺陷需要人工标注、专家审查或昂贵模拟等验证成本，而现有方法主要依赖单一模型的置信度分数，未能利用模型版本间预测、决策边界和局部邻域的变化信息，导致优先级排序效果有限。

Method: 提出RiskBlend框架，这是一个分类器无关的优先级排序框架，融合四种互补风险信号：历史失败模式、预测偏移、决策边界偏移和邻域变化。采用验证集学习的APFD平方加权来组合这些信号，实现对回归缺陷的有效优先级排序。

Result: 在四个数据集、五个分类器、四种回归更新场景和15个随机种子共计1200个实验配置中，RiskBlend在所有80个数据集-分类器-场景组合中取得最高平均APFD，较最强基线提升最高达0.32。基于置信度的方法仅在稀疏类别特征的线性分类器上有竞争力，这归因于特征空间几何特性。

Conclusion: 跨版本行为信号为机器学习系统中的回归缺陷优先级排序提供了重要的互补信息。RiskBlend通过融合多种版本间变化信号，在广泛的实验设置中稳定超越现有方法，验证了该框架的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: When machine learning classifiers are retrained, inputs correctly classified by the previous model version may be misclassified by the updated version, creating regression faults that are costly to detect because verifying predictions against ground truth may require human annotation, expert review, or expensive simulation rather than inexpensive model inference. Test input prioritization addresses this problem by ranking inputs so that a limited verification budget reveals as many regression faults as possible. Existing approaches rely predominantly on single-model confidence scores and do not exploit how predictions, decision boundaries, and local neighborhoods change between model versions. We propose RiskBlend, a classifier-agnostic prioritization framework that combines four complementary risk signals: historical failure patterns, prediction shift, decision-boundary shift, and neighborhood change. These signals are combined using validation-learned APFD-squared weighting. Across four datasets, five classifiers, four regression-update scenarios, and 15 random seeds, totaling 1,200 experimental configurations, RiskBlend achieves the highest average APFD in all 80 dataset-classifier-scenario combinations, with improvements of up to 0.32 APFD over the strongest baseline. Confidence-based methods remain competitive primarily for linear classifiers on sparse categorical features, which we attribute to feature-space geometry. The results show that cross-version behavioral signals provide important complementary information for prioritizing regression faults in machine learning systems.

</details>


### [247] [DART-FL: Burst-Aware Multitask Federated Learning under Dynamic Inference Demand at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27713)
*Yiming Xie,Pinrui Yu,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DART-FL，一个SLO感知、需求驱动的多任务联邦学习框架，在资源受限的边缘设备上联合优化推理-训练的资源配置与任务级训练重点，使高需求任务在爆发期间获得更多训练资源，提升推理准确率的同时保持长期多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统中模型训练和在线推理需要在资源受限设备上共存，且推理需求随时间在不同任务间动态变化。这带来两个耦合挑战：必须为推理预留足够算力以满足SLO，同时剩余训练容量应随任务需求自适应分配，使高频请求的任务能更早改善。现有方法未能联合解决这两个问题。

Method: DART-FL在每个调度间隔中：(1) 利用推理积压量和已配置的服务容量，确定推理所需的最小资源分配；(2) 将剩余训练容量通过队列感知的DPP启发式调度器在任务间分配；(3) 将任务分配映射为动态损失权重（loss weights），使高推理需求任务在早期通信轮次获得更大训练权重。客户端训练共享骨干网络加任务特定头部，通过FedAvg聚合完整多任务模型。

Result: 在Stanford Cars和Oxford Flowers 102数据集上，使用合成及真实阿里巴巴trace驱动的工作负载进行评估。结果显示DART-FL能动态调整推理-训练资源分配以应随时间变化的推理需求，并将高需求任务的学习进展导向其爆发期，在任务频繁请求时提升模型准确率，同时保持可比的长期多任务性能。

Conclusion: DART-FL通过联合优化推理-训练资源切分和任务级训练重点，有效解决了边缘场景下训练与推理共存且推理需求动态变化的问题，在高需求任务的关键时期显著提升服务质量，同时不牺牲多任务长期性能。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems increasingly require model training and online inference to coexist on resource-constrained devices, while inference demand can vary substantially across tasks over time. This creates two coupled challenges: sufficient computation must be reserved for inference to maintain service-level objectives (SLOs), while the remaining training capacity should adapt to task-specific demand so that frequently requested tasks can improve earlier during training.
  We propose an SLO-aware, demand-driven multitask federated learning framework (DART-FL) that jointly adapts the inference-training resource split and task-level training emphasis. At each scheduling interval, DART-FL uses the inference backlog and profiled service capacity to determine the minimum resource allocation required for inference. The remaining training capacity is then distributed across tasks using a queue-aware DPP-inspired scheduler, and the resulting task allocations are mapped to dynamic loss weights. This allows tasks experiencing higher inference demand to receive greater training emphasis in earlier communication rounds. Clients train a shared backbone with task-specific heads, and the complete multitask model is aggregated through FedAvg.
  We evaluate DART-FL using Stanford Cars and Oxford Flowers 102 under both synthetic and real Alibaba trace-derived workloads. Results show that DART-FL dynamically adapts the inference-training resource split to time-varying inference demand and shifts the learning progress of high-demand tasks toward their burst periods, improving model accuracy when those tasks are frequently requested while maintaining comparable long-term multitask performance.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond Non-IID: Learner--Client Distribution Mismatch in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27715)
*Yiming Xie,Lili Su,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中"客户端聚合数据分布与学习者目标分布不一致"的问题，提出了一种动态、影响力感知的客户端选择框架。该框架利用学习者持有的小型代理数据集，通过代理影响力信号（leave-one-out评估）估计各客户端对学习目标的潜在效用，从而优先选择最有信息量的数据源，同时控制统计噪声和数据异质性的负面影响。在CIFAR-10异构数据分区上的实验表明，该方法在收敛速度和精度上均优于静态和动态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习实践隐含假设聚合客户端数据分布能代表学习者目标分布，或所有客户端对学习者均匀有益，但这一假设在现实中往往不成立。传统客户端选择策略忽视了这种对齐偏差，而多源迁移学习的现有工作要么需要直接访问本地数据，要么仅使用一次性模型/特征聚合。本文旨在填补这一空白，理解和缓解学习者-客户端群体错配带来的负面影响。

Method: 提出一个动态、影响力感知的客户端选择框架。具体地：(1) 学习者持有小型代理数据集作为参考；(2) 利用代理影响力信号估计每个客户端对学习者优化目标的潜在效用；(3) 通过leave-one-out评估对客户端进行优先级排序；(4) 在选择过程中控制统计噪声和数据异质性带来的负面影响。框架能够捕捉客户端贡献随训练轮次动态变化的信息，以识别在多源迁移多样性下真正有益的来源。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上、异构数据划分条件下的实验表明，所提出的方法在收敛速度和最终精度上都一致优于静态和动态基线方法。实验还观察到客户端贡献在不同训练轮次中显著变化，验证了传统技术在多源迁移多样性下识别有益来源的不足。

Conclusion: 本文通过影响力感知的动态客户端选择策略有效解决了联邦学习中学习者与客户端群体分布错配的问题。该方法无需直接访问本地数据，仅依赖代理数据集和影响力信号即可实现更高效、更准确的联邦学习，为异构联邦环境下的客户端选择提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Federated learning systems are increasingly deployed to facilitate collaborative model training across a heterogeneous client population. Existing practice mostly implicitly assumes that the aggregated client data distribution is representative of the learner's target distribution or that learning from all available clients is uniformly beneficial for the learner distribution. However, such an assumption often does not hold in reality. Traditional client selection strategies in FL literature largely overlook such misalignment, while most existing work on multi-source transfer learning either requires direct access to local data or uses one-shot model/feature aggregation.
  In this paper, we take the initiative to understand and mitigate the impacts of such learner-client population misalignment. In particular, we consider the practical setting where the learner keeps a small proxy dataset. We observe that client contributions vary significantly across training rounds, and traditional technology is insufficient to identify beneficial sources under multi-source transfer diversity. Then, we propose a dynamic, influence-aware client selection framework that estimates each client's potential utility to the learner's optimization objective using proxy influence signals on a learner-specific proxy set. Via using leave-one-out evaluations, we prioritize the most informative sources of knowledge while controlling the negative impacts of statistical noise and data heterogeneity. Experiments on CIFAR-10 under heterogeneous data partitions demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms static and dynamic baselines, achieving faster convergence and higher accuracy.

</details>


### [249] [Leveraging a Foundation Model for the EEG-Based Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27719)
*Maggie Lin,Chung-Lin Hou,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用大规模脑电图预训练模型LaBraM提取高维潜在表征，并结合非线性随机森林分类器，在仅使用8秒EEG片段的情况下实现阿尔茨海默病（AD）的高精度诊断（ROC-AUC达89.36%），并验证了模型捕捉的临床生物标志物（枕额叶Alpha与Theta节律退化）与神经生理学指标（认知表现、临床严重程度、功率谱及非周期指数）显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD的生物异质性给传统线性诊断方法带来挑战，这类方法无法捕捉EEG中的非线性神经动力学特征，导致诊断精度受限。

Method: 采用在2,500+小时EEG数据上预训练的大型脑模型（LaBraM）提取高维潜在嵌入，结合非线性随机森林分类器进行痴呆患者与健康对照的区分；采用严格的受试者独立5折交叉验证；并进行事后遮挡分析和神经生理学对齐分析以验证临床有效性。

Result: ROC-AUC为89.36%±3.49%，PR AUC为81.45%±4.43%，平衡准确率为82.44%±4.34%，且仅需8秒EEG片段，性能优于频带功率和FOOOF参数化振荡特征等传统谱基线。遮挡分析确认模型捕捉枕额叶Alpha和Theta节律退化；LaBraM预测的痴呆概率与更差的认知表现、更高的临床严重程度、增加的theta和alpha相对功率及更高的非周期指数显著相关。

Conclusion: 深度潜在表征能够从噪声EEG信号中提取临床相关的病理特征，实现精准、快速且数据高效的AD诊断，验证了大规模预训练脑模型在神经疾病诊断中的潜力。

Abstract: Biological heterogeneity in Alzheimer's Disease (AD) poses a critical diagnostic challenge, particularly for traditional linear methods that fail to capture non-linear neural dynamics. To address this, we propose a diagnostic framework utilizing the Large Brain Model (LaBraM), pretrained on over 2,500 hours of EEG data. By integrating these high-dimensional latent embeddings with a non-linear Random Forest classifier, our approach effectively isolates robust disease markers. Under a rigorous subject-independent 5-fold cross-validation protocol, the method achieves an ROC-AUC of 89.36% +/- 3.49%, PR AUC of 81.45% +/- 4.43%, and Balanced Accuracy of 82.44% +/- 4.34% in distinguishing dementia patients from healthy controls. Notably, this performance uses only 8-second EEG segments, surpassing traditional spectral baselines, including band-power and parameterized oscillatory features (FOOOF). Post-hoc occlusion analysis confirms the model captures clinically validated biomarkers, specifically occipital-frontal Alpha and Theta rhythm degradation. Additional neurophysiological alignment analysis demonstrated that higher LaBraM-predicted dementia probability significantly correlated with worse cognitive performance, greater clinical severity, increased theta and alpha relative power, and higher aperiodic exponent. These findings demonstrate that deep latent representations extract clinically relevant signatures from noisy signals, enabling precise, rapid, and data-efficient diagnosis.

</details>


### [250] [Diffusion Distillation for Efficient Weather Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27728)
*Yiming Yang,Valentin Brekke,James Briant,Serge Guillas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督能量距离蒸馏的方法，将多步扩散模型教师压缩为单步学生模型，在全局天气预报和台风路径预测任务上优于现有蒸馏方法，且能以单次神经函数评估达到或超越教师模型的技能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量的天气集合预报，但迭代采样过程计算开销巨大，限制了其实际部署和应用效率。

Method: 提出监督能量距离蒸馏（supervised energy-distance distillation）方法，通过将学生模型的预测同时与教师模型的采样结果以及真实观测数据进行对齐（即最小化能量距离），将多步扩散教师压缩为单步学生。

Result: 在全局天气预报和台风路径预测实验中，该学生模型优于现有蒸馏方法，并在极端事件上保持了预报技能；仅需每个自回归步骤一次神经函数评估，即可在关键指标上达到或超越教师模型。

Conclusion: 监督能量距离蒸馏有效地解决了扩散式天气集合预报的计算瓶颈，在保持预测精度的同时大幅降低推理成本，为高分辨率天气集合预报的实时部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate skillful weather ensembles but require costly iterative sampling. We introduce a supervised energy-distance distillation method that compresses a multi-step diffusion teacher into a single-step student by aligning student forecasts with teacher samples and ground-truth observations. Experiments on global forecasting and typhoon-track prediction show that our student outperforms existing distillation methods and preserves skill for extreme events. It matches or surpasses the teacher across key metrics using only one neural function evaluation per autoregressive step.

</details>


### [251] [The Calls are Coming from Inside the Model: Investigating Probe-based Detection of Tool-Calling Errors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27750)
*Eric Yeats,Brendan Kennedy,Loc Truong,John Buckheit,Jung Lee,Jesse Friedbaum,John Emanuello,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了使用线性探针（linear probes）检测大型语言模型（LLM）错误工具调用（tool-calls）的有效性，在18个工具调用LLM上进行了评估，发现探针能有效捕获多种工具调用错误，包括标准日志框架难以记录的值正确但类型错误的参数错误，且探针能够泛化到新型错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的系统越来越多地与外部世界交互，检测工具的错误或不规范使用成为一个重要的安全问题。LLM的隐藏状态包含无法从输入输出中直接观察到的丰富信息，作者希望利用这一特性来检测错误的工具调用，特别是那些可能被标准日志框架遗漏的错误类型。

Method: 使用线性探针（linear probes）对LLM的隐藏状态进行分类，检测错误工具调用。在Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard基准上对18个工具调用LLM进行系统评估，考察模型规模、探测层位置和模型后训练类型等因素对探针效果的影响。

Result: 线性探针在检测多种工具调用错误方面表现出有效性，包括参数值错误但类型正确的错误（这类错误难以被标准日志框架记录）。模型大小、探测层选择和模型后训练类型是影响探针性能的关键因素。探针还能泛化到新型错误，这对实际部署至关重要。

Conclusion: 线性探针是检测LLM工具调用错误的有效手段，能够捕捉包括其他方法难以发现的错误类型，并且具备对新错误的泛化能力，为实际部署中的工具使用安全提供了有价值的检测方案。

Abstract: The hidden states of large language models (LLMs) are known to capture rich information relating to model knowledge and behavior that can be hard to extract from examination of input and output alone. As LLM-based systems increasingly interface with the external world, one area of concern is detecting incorrect or improper use of tools. Motivated by this, we study the effectiveness of using linear probes to detect incorrect tool-calls, measuring probe efficacy across 18 tool-calling LLMs evaluated on the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard. Overall, we find that probing is an effective means to catch a range of different tool-calling errors, including errors arising from using an argument that has the wrong value but the correct type, which might not be recorded by standard logging frameworks. Important factors in success include model size, probing layer, and model post-training type. We also show that probes are capable of generalizing to novel types of errors, which is critical in real world deployments.

</details>


### [252] [Beyond Search-Imitation: Prior-Directed Exploration for Searchless Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27757)
*Szymon Miłosz,Piotr Duch,Szymon Grabowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将反向KL熵正则替换为前向KL先验引导探索，并配合熵自适应采样温度，对搜索教师蒸馏得到的无搜索国际象棋网络进行自对弈强化学习微调，在保持棋力的同时提升战术准确率（谜题准确率93.9%→94.9%，杀四步准确率77%→81%），并发现战术准确率与棋力呈解离关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿搜索（MCTS）的教师蒸馏是无搜索棋类网络的常用训练方式，但模仿搜索结果并不能直接等价于拥有优秀的单步（forward pass）下棋能力。作者希望通过自对弈强化学习微调来直接优化单步棋力，同时需要解决RL探索机制的问题——传统熵奖励（反向KL到均匀分布）与均匀分布对齐，无法有效覆盖先验认为有希望的走法。

Method: 提出前向KL先导演化探索（prior-directed exploration）：将常规的反向KL奖励替换为网络自身MCTS先验的前向KL（mass-covering）散度，使探索覆盖先验判定有希望的走法；同时引入熵自适应采样温度，由价值头对结局的不确定性决定——局面已定时锐化温度。在约2000步内对Leela Chess Zero的Chessformer进行自对弈RL微调。

Result: 微调后谜题准确率从93.9%提升至94.9%（10万谜题集），杀四步准确率从77%升至81%，无搜索棋力保持或略高于基线。在匹配计算量的扫描中，战术增益集中在1个点带宽内而评级在基线上浮动（解离现象）；仅用谜题微调的对照组战术增益最大但棋力下降约260 Elo。分布级测量显示无正则化时自对弈坍缩为单条对弈线，前向KL先验在评分排行榜居首，与反向KL锚（集中两倍）统计持平但保留了更难的解。

Conclusion: 战术准确率与对弈棋力是可解离的两个维度——更强的谜题解决者不一定是更强的棋手；前向KL先引导探索能更充分地覆盖先验保留的走法，在维持棋力的同时提升短板战术，且其mass-covering特性保留了反向KL锚所丢弃的困难解。

Abstract: Searchless chess networks reach human master strength from a single forward pass by imitating a stronger teacher: the strongest, Leela Chess Zero's (Lc0) released Chessformer, distills the visit counts of an AlphaZero-style Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). Imitating a search is a poor proxy for playing without one, so we fine-tune for single-pass strength with self-play reinforcement learning (RL). Its exploration is usually supplied by an entropy bonus, the reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence to uniform. We replace it with a forward, mass-covering KL toward the network's own MCTS prior (prior-directed exploration), so exploration covers the moves the prior judges promising, and pair it with an entropy-adaptive sampling temperature, set by the value head's outcome uncertainty, that sharpens once a position is decided. In about two thousand steps it raises puzzle accuracy from 93.9% to 94.9% on a 100,000-puzzle suite and mate-in-four accuracy from 77% to 81% while holding searchless strength at or slightly above the base. Measuring tactical accuracy and playing strength together across a matched-compute sweep, we find the two dissociate: accuracy gains fall in a one-point band while ratings straddle the base, and a control fine-tuned on puzzles alone posts the study's largest tactical gains while shedding roughly 260 Elo; a better puzzle-solver is not thereby a stronger player. Distribution-level measurements show what anchoring buys: without a regularizer self-play collapses onto a single line of play, and the puzzles newly solved are the near misses whose winning move the prior kept alive. The forward-KL prior tops the rating ladder, statistically tied with a reverse-KL anchor that concentrates twice as hard and drops the hardest solutions the mass-covering prior keeps in support.

</details>


### [253] [Fast Weight Attention for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27763)
*Yifan Zhang,Steve Ta,Jasper Zhang,Jichen Feng,Shuzhen Li,Yongxin Zhang,Yifeng Liu,Huizhuo Yuan,Mengdi Wang,Quanquan Gu,Andrew Chi-Chih Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了循环快速权重记忆与选择性状态空间模型中的在线学习规则，在自回归"先写后读"语义下，提出了前缀预测目标的更新形式，并推导出归一化一阶更新算法家族（Falcon-1/2/3及其A变体），提供了循环、掩码并行和分块并行三种实现形式以及数值稳定的正衰减重归一化方法，在语言建模和变长数字加法任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环快速权重记忆和选择性状态空间模型将不断扩展的上下文压缩为固定大小的循环状态，其状态转移本质上是一个在线学习规则。然而，现有研究对同一时间步关联(t)与时间步t处的前缀对齐样本的处理不加区分，导致内部优化目标不明确。本文旨在从理论上厘清在"先写后读"自回归语义下，前缀预测目标所对应的正确局部学习样本，并系统推导不同目标函数下的归一化更新规则。

Method: 本文首先指出，在前缀预测目标下，时间步t暴露的局部样本应为前缀对齐的对(x_t,y_t)=(φ(k_{t-1}),v_t)，而非常见的同一步关联(φ(k_t),v_t)。然后针对平方误差回归和负内积两种目标分别推导归一化一阶更新规则：回归家族包括Falcon-1（标量NLMS更新）、Falcon-2（逐列扩展）和Falcon-3（滑动窗口小批量更新）；对应的内积变体为Falcon-1A/2A/3A。作者给出了循环、掩码并行和分块并行三种可计算形式，并引入了数值稳定的正衰减重归一化技术。

Result: 代表性变体在语言建模任务上与现有方法具有竞争力，并在变长数字加法任务上显著改善了长度外推能力。实验结果表明，该系统化框架有效解耦了循环序列模型中的时间对齐、可塑性、遗忘和有界排练等关键机制，为理解在线学习规则在递归架构中的作用提供了统一的视角。

Conclusion: 本文为循环快速权重记忆提供了一套系统化的理论基础和算法家族，明确了前缀预测目标下的正确局部学习信号，并提出了一系列可高效并行实现的归一化更新规则。该框架不仅具有理论清晰性，也在实践中验证了语言建模和外推性能，为未来研究不同遗忘机制、学习率和排练策略在递归序列模型中的协同作用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recurrent fast-weight memories and selective state-space models compress an expanding context into a fixed-size recurrent state, making the state transition an online learning rule. We study this rule under read-after-write autoregressive semantics. For the prefix-prediction objective considered here, the local fast-memory example revealed at step $t$ is the prefix-aligned pair $(\mathbf{x}_t,\mathbf{y}_t)=(φ(\mathbf{k}_{t-1}),\mathbf{v}_t)$. The common same-step association $(φ(\mathbf{k}_t),\mathbf{v}_t)$ remains causal, but optimizes a different internal objective. We derive normalized first-order updates for squared-error regression and negative inner-product objectives. The regression family comprises Falcon-1 (a scalar NLMS update), Falcon-2 (its per-column extension), and Falcon-3 (a sliding-window mini-batch update); Falcon-1A/Falcon-2A/Falcon-3A are the corresponding inner-product variants. We provide recurrent, masked-parallel, and chunk-parallel forms, together with numerically stable positive-decay renormalization. Representative variants remain competitive in language modeling and improve length extrapolation on variable-digit addition. This framework separates temporal alignment, plasticity, forgetting, and bounded rehearsal in recurrent sequence models.

</details>


### [254] [Initialization Is Critical: Advancing Federated Short-Term Load Forecasting under Load Heterogeneity via Model Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27791)
*Jianing Chen,Vajiheh Farhadi,Yan Li,Thomas La Porta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习在短期负荷预测（STLF）中面临的结构性异质性（客户对外部因素响应不同和时序负荷特征不同）问题，从模型初始化角度提出两种策略：全局预训练初始化和局部序列初始化（SLIAvg），在不改变联邦学习框架的前提下有效降低客户漂移、改善收敛性并降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期负荷预测依赖细粒度的智能电表数据，但直接共享数据存在隐私风险；联邦学习虽提供隐私保护框架，然而客户的负荷数据存在结构性异质性（不同的外生因素响应和不同的时间序列特征），这会降低联邦学习下的预测性能，现有工作对该问题关注不足。

Method: 本文从模型初始化角度提出两种策略：（1）全局视角：当有辅助公共负荷数据可用时，采用预训练初始化策略，在联邦训练前初始化全局模型以减少训练过程中的客户漂移；（2）局部视角：提出SLIAvg（顺序局部初始化策略），在每轮通信中允许参与客户从逐步适配的模型出发，促进更一致的训练过程。这两种策略仅修改初始化过程，因此与现有FL框架和隐私增强技术兼容。

Result: 在真实智能电表数据上的实验表明，基于两种代表性预测架构，所提出的初始化策略能有效提升预测性能，具体表现为客户漂移减少、收敛行为改善以及预测误差降低。

Conclusion: 模型初始化是缓解联邦STLF中结构性异质性问题的有效途径，全局预训练初始化和局部序列初始化（SLIAvg）两种策略均能显著提升联邦短期负荷预测的准确性和稳定性，且具有广泛兼容性。

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting (STLF) provides essential information for numerous applications in modern power systems. However, accurate STLF often relies on fine-grained smart-meter data from distributed users, raising increasing concerns about data privacy. Federated learning (FL) has therefore emerged as a promising privacy-preserving paradigm for STLF. Nevertheless, this paper reveals structured heterogeneity in clients' load data. Specifically, clients exhibit different responses to exogenous factors and distinct temporal load profiles, which can degrade forecasting performance in FL. To mitigate these issues, this paper studies the role of model initialization in federated STLF, and proposes two initialization strategies from global and local perspectives. For global model initialization, when auxiliary public load data are available, a pretrained initialization strategy is developed to initialize the global model before federated training, thereby reducing client drift during the training process. For local model initialization, we propose SLIAvg, a sequential local initialization strategy that promotes a more consistent training process by allowing participating clients to start from progressively adapted models within each communication round. Since the proposed strategies only modify the initialization process, they are compatible with most existing FL frameworks and privacy-enhancing techniques. Experiments on real smart-meter data with two representative forecasting architectures demonstrate that the proposed strategies effectively improve forecasting performance, as evidenced by reduced client drift, improved convergence behavior, and lower forecasting errors.

</details>


### [255] [Node-wise Feature Encoding for Neural Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27794)
*Matthew Grenier,William Hammer,Andrew Heuer,Nikhil Krishna,Yi Wang,Ramtin Zand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FeatureFormer，一种将节点级计算成本（FLOPs、参数量、内存代理）显式编码到门控图注意力架构中的神经性能预测器，并引入大规模能耗数据集NNEQ，在延迟和能耗预测上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN和Transformer的性能预测器在神经架构搜索中表现优异，但忽略了节点级计算成本，限制了其对性能关键操作（如卷积、池化等）的建模能力。在资源受限的边缘设备场景下，准确预测延迟和能耗至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出FeatureFormer：在设计空间中融入显式的节点级编码（FLOPs、参数量、内存代理），并采用门控图注意力（gated graph attention）架构进行性能预测。2) 构建NNEQ：一个新的大规模能耗数据集，支持延迟和能耗的统一评估。

Result: FeatureFormer在延迟和能耗预测两个指标上均达到最先进水平，在具有挑战性的域外（out-of-domain）场景中同样表现优异。此外，所提出的节点级编码方法具有通用性，能以极小的开销稳定提升现有预测器的性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模节点级计算成本能显著提升神经性能预测器的准确性与泛化能力，且该编码方案可无缝迁移至其他预测架构。

Abstract: As neural networks are increasingly deployed on resource constrained edge devices, accurate prediction of latency and energy is critical for efficient neural architecture search. Existing GNN and transformer based predictors achieve strong results but largely ignore node-level computational cost, limiting their ability to model performance critical operations. To address this, we introduce FeatureFormer, a neural performance predictor that incorporates explicit node-wise encodings of FLOPs, parameter counts, and memory proxies within a gated graph attention architecture. We also present NNEQ, a new large-scale energy consumption dataset that enables unified evaluation of latency and energy prediction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FeatureFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance across both metrics, including challenging out-of-domain settings. Finally, we show that the proposed encoding is broadly applicable and consistently improves existing predictors with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [256] [Actionable CBFI: Integrating Structural Decomposition and Causal Counterfactual Recourse for Tabular Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27821)
*Sejong Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A-CBFI（可操作的基于案例的特征重要性），一个基于结构因果模型的诊断-处方一体化框架，用于表格机器学习模型的可操作反事实干预。它通过识别协同交互瓶颈并释放抑制性结构锁，将超过98.3%的干预努力集中在已诊断的根因上，在金融和医疗领域的实验中使主动人类干预负担减少76.9%，同时保持与穷举因果基线相当的全局反事实成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI中的反事实干预方法面临三大挑战：因果无效性、过度认知负担和预测失败。穷举因果搜索需要修改多个属性，而基于加性归因的SHAP方法忽略高阶特征协同效应，在XGBoost等复杂非线性模型上导致预测动力不足和干预效果分散。

Method: A-CBFI是一个诊断-处方集成框架。它基于结构因果模型（SCMs），隔离协同交互瓶颈并释放抑制性结构锁，将其转化为针对性干预。该方法在数学上将主动用户干预空间（L_active）与下游效应分离，将干预努力集中在已诊断的根因上。

Result: 在金融和医疗领域的实证评估中，A-CBFI将主动人类干预负担减少76.9%，同时保持与穷举因果基线相当的全局反事实成本，并实现了所有因果可行实例的完全相对收敛。

Conclusion: A-CBFI通过优先处理已诊断的因果瓶颈，在保持因果有效性的同时提供针对性和可操作的反事实干预，解决了现有方法在因果有效性、认知负担和预测效果之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) increasingly calls for actionable counterfactual recourse, yet current methodologies face challenges related to causal invalidity, excessive cognitive burden, and predictive failure. Exhaustive causal search algorithms often require modifications to multiple attributes, whereas additive attribution-guided methods, such as SHAP, ignore higher-order feature synergies, leading to suboptimal predictive momentum and diffuse intervention effort in complex nonlinear models, such as XGBoost. To bridge this gap, we introduce actionable case-based feature importance (A-CBFI), a diagnosis-prescription integrated framework for tabular machine learning. Grounded in structural causal models (SCMs), A-CBFI isolates synergistic interaction bottlenecks and releases suppressive structural locks, translating them into targeted interventions. By mathematically separating the active user intervention space (L_{\mathrm{active}}) from downstream effects and concentrating over 98.3% of the intervention effort on diagnosed root causes, A-CBFI enables highly targeted interventions. Empirical evaluations across the financial and healthcare domains demonstrate that A-CBFI reduces the active human intervention burden by 76.9% while maintaining comparable global recourse cost to exhaustive causal baselines. By prioritizing the diagnosed causal bottlenecks, A-CBFI provides targeted and actionable recourse while maintaining causal validity and achieving full relative convergence across all causally feasible instances.

</details>


### [257] [FedEHR-Agents: Federated Agentic Optimization for Automated EHR Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27856)
*Jun Bai,Ruilin Wang,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FedEHR-Agents，一种以经验为中心的联邦式自主代理优化框架，用于自动化电子健康记录（EHR）建模。通过在各医院部署自主临床EHR代理进行数据预处理和模型开发，并由联邦服务器进行证据引导的经验聚合，将分散的临床建模经验整合为全局元提示，超越了传统以参数为中心的联邦学习范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习在临床EHR建模协作中主要是模型中心化的，仅共享预测模型或其更新，忽略了自主代理在建模过程中积累的丰富临床建模经验。同时，医院间的直接协作受限于患者级数据的敏感性，需要一种能够在隐私保护前提下实现更深层次协作的新范式。

Method: 1) 每个医院部署自主临床EHR代理，执行数据预处理和模型开发，并通过历史记忆、任务特定评估和TextGrad提示精炼来迭代优化本地临床建模经验；2) 联邦服务器执行证据引导的经验聚合，跨异构医院整合可靠且互补的建模经验；3) 将聚合经验蒸馏为全局元提示，供各医院进行后续本地精炼。

Result: 在真实的多医院EHR基准数据集上，FedEHR-Agents在多种临床预测任务中一致优于本地和联邦基线，并在不同联邦规模和LLM骨干网络下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作证实临床建模经验是一种有前景的联邦协作对象，超越了传统参数中心化的FL范式，为联邦自主临床智能指明了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models are enabling autonomous clinical agents to perform increasingly complex electronic health record (EHR) modeling workflows. However, agents deployed at individual hospitals remain constrained by institution-specific data and modeling environments, while direct cross-hospital collaboration is restricted by the sensitivity of patient-level EHR data. Although federated learning (FL) provides a natural foundation for privacy-preserving collaboration, existing approaches remain predominantly model-centric, limiting federation to prediction models or their updates while overlooking the richer modeling experience accumulated by autonomous agents. To address this limitation, we propose FedEHR-Agents, an experience-centric federated agentic optimization framework for automated EHR modeling. Each hospital deploys an autonomous clinical EHR agent that performs data preprocessing and model development while refining local clinical modeling experience through historical memory, task-specific evaluation, and TextGrad-based prompt refinement. The federated server performs evidence-guided experience aggregation to integrate reliable and complementary modeling experience across heterogeneous hospitals and distills the aggregated experience into global meta-prompts for subsequent local refinement. Extensive experiments on real-world multi-hospital EHR benchmarks demonstrate that FedEHR-Agents consistently outperforms local and federated baselines across diverse clinical prediction tasks and remains robust across different federation scales and LLM backbones. These results establish clinical modeling experience as a promising collaborative object beyond conventional parameter-centric FL and point toward federated autonomous clinical intelligence.

</details>


### [258] [SOMTab: Set-Order Mamba for Efficient Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27882)
*Hao Wang,Siyu Zhang,Wei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOMTab，一种基于Set-Order Mamba架构的表格上下文学习基础模型。它仅在最终预测阶段使用注意力机制，而用Mamba状态空间混合来构建行列表示，同时提出DCH-TailMix合成先验来生成多样化的依赖结构。实验表明SOMTab在接近Transformer基础模型性能的同时，推理速度更快、GPU内存占用更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型几乎完全依赖注意力机制贯穿整个建模流程，作者质疑这种设计是否在每个阶段都必要，探索能否在表示构建阶段用更高效的机制替代注意力，从而在保持性能的同时提升效率。

Method: SOMTab将表示构建与查询条件检索分离：1）行/列表示阶段，将无序表格token映射为稳定的潜在槽，并用基于Mamba的状态空间混合构建紧凑表示；2）最终预测阶段保留注意力机制以维持查询条件的上下文检索能力。另提出DCH-TailMix合成先验，结合度修正图异质性与混合重尾分布以增强合成依赖结构的多样性。

Result: 在多个表格基准上，SOMTab性能接近强Transformer表格基础模型，同时推理速度更快、GPU内存占用更低，实现了更优的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 注意力并非表格上下文学习每个阶段都必需的；通过将高效的Mamba状态空间混合用于表示构建、注意力仅保留在最终预测阶段，可以在性能和效率之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models based on in-context learning have recently emerged as strong alternatives to task-specific model fitting. However, the current performance frontier remains dominated by attention-heavy architectures, where attention is used throughout the modeling pipeline. This raises a natural question: is attention necessary at every stage of tabular in-context learning? We introduce SOMTab, a Set-Order Mamba architecture for efficient tabular in-context learning. SOMTab separates representation construction from query-conditioned retrieval. For row and column representations, it maps unordered table tokens into stable latent slots and applies Mamba-based state-space mixing to construct compact representations. For final prediction, it retains attention-based in-context learning to preserve query-conditioned retrieval from labeled context examples. We further introduce DCH-TailMix, a synthetic prior that combines degree-corrected graph heterogeneity with mixed heavy-tailed regimes to diversify synthetic dependency structures. Across tabular benchmarks, SOMTab approaches the performance of strong Transformer-based tabular foundation models while achieving faster inference and lower GPU memory usage, yielding a favorable efficiency--accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [259] [Beyond Pairwise Graphs in Science: Hypergraph Adaptive Wavelet Operators for Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27883)
*Rajat Sarkar,Venkataramana Runkana,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO（Hypergraph Adaptive waveLet Operator），一种将物理系统求解映射到超图谱小波域进行学习的神经算子架构。HALO通过Chebyshev多项式小波滤波器避免超图拉普拉斯特征分解，以线性稀疏矩阵代价获得局部化谱核，并通过可训练的二进小波尺度实现自适应频响。在2D和3D结构化及非结构化离散化基准上取得最优或接近最优精度，并能稳定多步自回归滚动，在数十万点的工业气动网格上优于或持平最强固定离散化Transformer模型，同时保持分辨率等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统常由解算子建模，但学习这些映射极具挑战，尤其对于需要历史信息同化并在自回归滚动中保持稳定的时变系统。现有神经算子主要受限于规则结构化网格，而真实模拟常需非结构化网格或点云以解析复杂几何。图神经算子虽能处理此类域，但成对边无法直接捕捉网格单元、局部邻域或守恒体之间的群组耦合。因此需要一种能同时处理非结构化几何、捕捉高阶群组交互、且稳定可扩展的神经算子架构。

Method: HALO将物理域提升为超图结构，并在其谱小波域中学习。关键设计包括：(1) 使用Chebyshev多项式构造谱小波滤波器，避免显式的超图拉普拉斯特征分解，仅需线性稀疏矩阵乘法成本；(2) 可训练的二进小波尺度，通过正则化使其趋向紧框架覆盖，使频率响应能自适应每个PDE的同时鼓励稳定的多尺度谱覆盖；(3) 无需固定离散化，保持分辨率等变性，可处理任意网格/点云拓扑。

Result: 在2D和3D的结构化及非结构化离散化基准测试中，HALO在频率方法、Transformer、DeepONet、状态空间模型和图基方法等基线中取得最佳或接近最佳精度，并在多步自回归滚动中保持稳定。在具有数十万点的工业气动几何网格上，HALO以单一模型达到或超过最强固定离散化Transformer的性能，同时保持分辨率等变性。

Conclusion: HALO通过超图小波域学习有效解决了复杂几何上神经算子的关键瓶颈：高阶群组耦合建模、非结构化域自适应和稳定长程滚动。其线性稀疏矩阵代价和分辨率等变性使其适合大规模工业应用，在精度和稳定性上全面优于或持平现有最强基线。

Abstract: Physical systems are often modeled by solution operators that map input fields, parameters, geometries, or past states to steady or future physical states. Learning these maps is difficult, especially for time-dependent systems that must assimilate history and remain stable under autoregressive rollout. Many neural operators work best on regular, structured grids, while realistic simulations often require unstructured meshes or point clouds to resolve complex geometries; in such settings, grid-centric representations can lose accuracy. Graph neural operators handle these domains through message passing or spectral graph filtering, but pairwise edges do not directly capture group-wise couplings among mesh cells, local neighborhoods, or conservation volumes. We introduce the Hypergraph Adaptive waveLet Operator (HALO), which lifts the domain to a hypergraph and learns in its spectral wavelet domain. HALO avoids explicit hypergraph-Laplacian eigendecomposition through Chebyshev polynomial wavelet filters, giving localized spectral kernels at linear sparse-matrix cost. Its trainable dyadic wavelet scales are regularized toward tight-frame coverage, allowing the frequency response to adapt to each PDE while encouraging stable multi-scale spectral coverage. Across 2D and 3D benchmarks on structured and unstructured discretizations, HALO achieves best or near-best accuracy among frequency-, transformer-, DeepONet-, state-space-, and graph-based baselines and sustains stable multi-step rollouts. The same model scales to industrial aerodynamic geometries: on meshes of a few hundred thousand points it is on par with, or better than, the strongest fixed-discretization transformers, while remaining resolution-equivariant.

</details>


### [260] [There and Back Again: Bidirectional Diffusion Bridges for Multimodality Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27885)
*Gabe Guo,Elon Litman,Thanawat Sornwanee,Jose Blanchet,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BIT是一种双向图像-文本扩散桥接模型，直接从文本出发插值到图像，实现了源感知的生成路径和端点条件化的双向生成框架（文本到图像及图像到文本），通过随机微积分推导出SDE形式，在多个视觉-语言和自然科学评估中优于去噪扩散和确定性流基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态翻译（如文本到图像）方法存在两个局限：(1) 生成路径没有直接表示源模态，限制了某些采样算法的灵活性；(2) 单向生成，无法进行反向映射（如图像到文本）。这促使研究者探索一种既能直接利用源模态信息、又能支持双向转换的统一生成框架。

Method: BIT通过随机微积分推导出图像与文本之间的扩散桥（diffusion bridge），形成可模拟的SDE形式，并设计可扩展到高维的可处理损失函数。该方法从文本直接插值到图像，实现源感知的生成路径；同时通过端点条件化过程支持从图像到文本的逆向遍历，统一为双向生成框架。

Result: 实验表明BIT与去噪扩散和确定性流基线相比具有竞争力，并在多项视觉-语言及自然科学评估任务上优于这些基线方法。

Conclusion: BIT通过随机微积分构造双向扩散桥，克服了现有跨模态翻译在采样灵活性和方向性上的局限，提供了一种统一的双向多模态生成框架，在多个评测任务上达到或超越现有方法。

Abstract: Multimodality translation (e.g., text-to-image) is a core generative AI task. However, existing approaches (1) follow generative paths that do not directly represent the source modality, limiting the flexibility of some sampling algorithms; and (2) are unidirectional, preventing inversion (e.g., image-to-text). We propose BIT: Bidirectional Image-Text Diffusion Bridges. In contrast to previous approaches, BIT starts directly from text and interpolates into images, providing (1) a source-aware generative path that enables diverse and flexible sampling algorithms; and (2) an endpoint-conditioned process that can be traversed from image to text, providing a unified, bidirectional generative framework. BIT is derived through stochastic calculus, yielding SDE forms amenable to simulation and tractable loss functions that scale to high dimensions. Our experiments show that BIT is competitive with denoising-diffusion and deterministic-flow baselines, and outperforms them on several vision--language and natural-science evaluations.

</details>


### [261] [TACIT-Switch: Cost-Aware Model Escalation for LLM Agents from Censored Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27911)
*Ji'an Lei,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACIT-SWITCH是一种基于区间删失干预时间（TACIT）的混合治愈阈值模型，通过学习永久切换策略来决定何时将小语言模型智能体路由到大语言模型智能体，在保持低成本的同时提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型智能体成本低但容易陷入持久性故障模式，大语言模型智能体更可靠但成本高。这种可靠性与成本的权衡促使研究者探索路由方法，即在何时将任务切换到更大的模型。现有路由方法在执行前、固定轨迹前缀或局部步骤决策，需要更优的切换策略。

Method: TACIT-SWITCH从累积轨迹证据和教师标注的区间删失干预时间中学习永久切换策略。它将每个标注表示为累积风险尺度上的区间删失观测，构建混合治愈阈值模型，估计配对强模型成功概率以及成功条件下的切换阈值。部署时无需教师参与。

Result: 在机制化多步仿真中，TACIT-SWITCH在可比成本下比任务级、步骤级和固定前缀路由基线提升成功率7.4-11.1个百分点。在ALFWorld上达到48.5%（4B Cheap）和45.5%（9B Cheap）的坚持率，在DABench上达到73.1%，均为学习策略中的最高坚持率。

Conclusion: TACIT-SWITCH通过将教师标注建模为区间删失的混合治愈阈值模型，有效利用了任务特征与累积轨迹风险的互补信息，在多个基准上实现了成本与可靠性的更优平衡。

Abstract: Agents with smaller language-model backbones are less expensive but can drift into persistent failure modes, whereas those with larger backbones are generally more reliable but more costly. This reliability-cost trade-off motivates routing methods that decide when to invoke an agent with a larger backbone: before execution, after a fixed trajectory prefix, or locally at individual steps. Our method, TACIT-SWITCH, learns permanent handoff policies from accumulated trajectory evidence and Teacher-Annotated Censored Intervention Times (TACIT). It represents each annotation as an interval-censored observation on a cumulative-risk scale. The resulting mixture-cure threshold model estimates the probability that the paired Strong rollout succeeds and, conditional on success, the handoff threshold; no teacher is required at deployment. In a mechanism-based multi-step simulation, TACIT-SWITCH improves success by 7.4-11.1 percentage points over task-level, step-level, and fixed-prefix routing baselines at comparable cost. Within that controlled simulation, ablations show that task features and cumulative trajectory risk provide complementary information. With operating points selected on development data, TACIT-SWITCH achieves the highest held-out success among learned policies on both ALFWorld (48.5% with 4B Cheap; 45.5% with 9B Cheap) and DABench (73.1%).

</details>


### [262] [TI$^2$PS: A Topology-Informed Inverse Design Framework for Stochastic Multicellular Pattern Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27931)
*Kenji Komiya,Andrew Kailiang Jin,Ryo Nishikimi,Kunio Kashino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Betti向量（拓扑数据分析）与逆代理建模的新型框架，用于从目标多细胞空间模式中估计基于智能体模型（ABM）的细胞级参数，并在斑马鱼色素图案形成的基准测试中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多细胞ABM参数估计面临两大挑战：一是细胞级参数（智能体特定变量）难以直接估计；二是随机细胞增殖和死亡条件下，难以定量评估多细胞排列的拓扑特征。现有方法如PointNet++需要大量训练数据且效果有限。

Method: 该框架整合两种方法：(1) 通过拓扑数据分析（TDA）获取Betti向量，用于一致地表示多种多细胞空间配置的拓扑特征；(2) 采用逆代理建模（inverse surrogate modeling），直接从目标模式推断对应的ABM参数。

Result: 在斑马鱼色素图案形成（多细胞相互作用驱动的图案形成的代表性模型）上验证了该框架的有效性。结果表明该方法能成功估计ABM参数，并在所有评估指标上优于PointNet++。值得注意的是，仅使用10%训练数据的本方法，在各项指标上均优于使用100%数据的PointNet++。

Conclusion: 提出的框架在数据效率和估计精度上均显著优于现有方法，为解决多细胞ABM参数估计问题提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel framework to estimate parameters for reproducing target multicellular patterns using an agent-based model (ABM). Two major challenges in multicellular ABMs are estimating cell-level parameters (agent-specific variables) and quantitatively evaluating the topological characteristics of multicellular arrangements under stochastic cell proliferation and death. To address these challenges, we integrate two approaches: Betti vectors and inverse surrogate modeling. The Betti vectors obtained through topological data analysis can consistently represent features of a wide range of multicellular spatial configurations. The inverse surrogate modeling enables direct inference of the corresponding ABM parameters from the target patterns. We validated the proposed framework using zebrafish pigment pattern formation, a representative model of pattern formation driven by multicellular interactions. The results demonstrate that our framework successfully estimates ABM parameters and outperforms conventional methods such as PointNet++. Notably, the proposed method, which used only 10% of the training data, outperformed PointNet++, which used 100% of the data, across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [263] [Temporal Memory-Aware Online Test-Time Adaptation on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27948)
*Bo Li,Xin Zheng,Ming Jin,Can Wang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DGOTTA框架，首次将在线测试时自适应(TTA)应用于动态图上的动态图神经网络(DGNN)，通过时间感知增强、记忆感知模型预测和一致性引导的在线自适应三个模块，解决动态图上的分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图测试时自适应研究仅限于静态图，而动态图中结构连接和节点语义随时间持续演化，导致分布偏移更为复杂，使得动态图神经网络在测试阶段性能下降且难以自适应，目前尚无相关工作填补这一空白。

Method: 提出DGOTTA框架，包含三个核心模块：(1)时间感知增强模块，扩展测试动态图的多样性以应对复杂的时空偏移；(2)记忆感知模型预测模块，缓解灾难性遗忘问题；(3)一致性引导的在线自适应模块，强制实现时间对齐和记忆平滑性。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集和四个DGNN骨干模型上的大量实验表明，DGOTTA在多种分布偏移场景和多种模型架构下显著提升了泛化性能。

Conclusion: DGOTTA有效填补了动态图上测试时自适应的研究空白，通过三个协作模块实现了对动态图分布偏移的鲁棒自适应，验证了其在真实场景下的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) on graphs aims to adapt a graph neural network (GNN) that is well-trained on the training graph to the test graph, which involves potential distribution shifts that may harm model generalization and test-time inference. While recent efforts have investigated TTA on static graphs, there is still a research gap on dynamic graphs learned with dynamic GNN (DGNN) models, where both structural connectivity and node semantics evolve continuously over time. This makes adapting a DGNN model for reliable test-time performance substantially challenging. To fill this gap, in this work, we propose a novel framework of temporal memory-aware Online Test-Time Adaptation on Dynamic Graphs, named DGOTTA, to effectively adapt well-trained DGNNs during test time. Specifically, the proposed DGOTTA contains three modules: (1) temporal-aware augmentation, to extend the diversity of test dynamic graphs for addressing complex temporal and spatial shifts; (2) memory-aware model prediction, to alleviate catastrophic forgetting; (3) consistency-guided online adaptation, to enforce temporal alignment and memory smoothness. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets and four DGNN backbones demonstrate that DGOTTA significantly improves generalization under diverse distribution shifts and multiple model architectures.

</details>


### [264] [PhyMamba: Physics-Modulated Mamba for Robust Battery Health Prognostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27978)
*Sara Sameer,Yunyi Zhao,Wei Zhang,Minggang Zeng,Wenqing Li,Man-Fai Ng,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhyMamba，一种将电化学老化机理融入序列建模的两阶段物理调制Mamba框架，用于电池健康长期预测，在三个公开数据集上相比多种基线实现平均误差降低31.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池健康预测是电池管理系统（BMS）的核心功能，但长期健康预测因运行工况依赖性和传感器噪声而具有挑战性。现有方法往往依赖侵入式测量以显式识别内部老化参数，限制了实际部署。

Method: PhyMamba采用两阶段架构：阶段1中，轻量级Mamba编码器处理BMS信号，生成潜在表示，并通过老化参数化模块转化为物理信息老化特征；阶段2中，定制化Mamba预测主干执行多周期预测，物理机制被紧密集成以调节模型内部时间更新，使其朝退化一致的方向演化。该方法无需显式识别内部老化参数。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、多种预测视野下，PhyMamba取得最佳综合性能，平均误差相比多种基线降低31.8%，同时提供了优化的精度-效率权衡，支持实际部署。

Conclusion: PhyMamba验证了将电化学老化物理信息融入Mamba序列模型的有效性，在无需侵入式参数识别的前提下实现了更优的预测精度和部署效率，为电池健康管理提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Battery health prognostics is a core function in battery management systems (BMSs), yet long-horizon health forecasting from BMS signals remains challenging due to operating-condition dependency and sensor noise. In this paper, we propose PhyMamba, a two-stage physics-modulated Mamba framework that integrates electrochemical aging into sequence modelling. PhyMamba does not require explicit identification of internal aging parameters, which often relies on intrusive measurements. In stage-1, a lightweight Mamba encoder first processes BMS signals and produces a latent representation that is transformed via an aging parameterization module, into physics-informed aging features. In stage-2, a customized Mamba forecasting backbone performs multi-cycle prediction, where physics is tightly integrated to regulate the model's internal temporal updates toward degradation-consistent evolution. Experiments on three public datasets under multiple forecast horizons show that PhyMamba achieves the best aggregated performance, with an overall mean error reduction of 31.8% compared with a diverse range of baselines. PhyMamba also offers an optimized accuracy-efficiency trade-off, which supports practical deployment for robust battery health prognostics.

</details>


### [265] [Is Monte Carlo Tree Search Just Every-Visit Monte Carlo Control?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27985)
*Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上论证了蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）与逐次访问蒙特卡洛（every-visit MC）控制在轨迹生成和动作值更新层面本质上等价，MCTS只是用搜索语言和数据结构表述的every-visit MC控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS和every-visit MC控制通常被视为两种不同的方法，分别用搜索语言（选择、扩展、模拟、回传）和强化学习语言（轨迹采样、回报估计、动作值更新、策略改进）来描述。作者希望揭示两者在核心机制上的等价性，消除术语造成的误解。

Method: 作者通过概念映射的方式建立等价关系：将树策略与rollout策略视为单一演化策略中已学习和未学习的部分；将扩展操作对应首次访问与初始化；将回传操作对应普通的逐次访问蒙特卡洛更新。在此解释下，MCTS的四个阶段简化为两个基本操作：当前策略下的轨迹采样和逐次访问蒙特卡洛更新。

Result: 文章表明MCTS实际上就是every-visit Monte Carlo控制，只不过使用了搜索的语言和数据表示形式来表达。这一等价性旨在帮助读者更清晰地理解两种方法的内在联系。

Conclusion: 本文是一篇说明性（expository）笔记，旨在明确展示MCTS与every-visit MC控制之间的等价关系，使这一等价性更容易被识别和理解。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and every-visit Monte Carlo (MC) control are usually presented as different methods. MCTS is described in the language of search (selection, expansion, simulation, and backup), whereas MC control is described in the language of reinforcement learning (trajectory sampling, return estimation, action-value updating, and policy improvement). This note argues that, at the level of trajectory generation and action-value updating, the distinction is largely terminological. The tree policy and rollout policy can be viewed as the learned and not-yet-learned parts of a single evolving policy; expansion corresponds to first visit and initialization; and backup is the ordinary every-visit Monte Carlo update. Under this interpretation, the four stages of MCTS reduce to two basic operations: trajectory sampling under the current policy and every-visit Monte Carlo updating. In this sense, MCTS is simply every-visit Monte Carlo control expressed in the language and data structure of search. The purpose of this note is expository: to make this equivalence explicit and easier to recognize.

</details>


### [266] [A Method for Layer Bit-Width Allocation in LLM Quantization via Performance Maximization Under a Quality-Degradation Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28003)
*Artem Safronov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对Gemma-3-1B的分层比特分配方法，将问题形式化为在给定质量退化预算约束下的性能最大化（延迟降低）。基于先前的SA-PTQ层敏感性分析，在TensorRT-LLM中逐层确定精度（FFN、Attention、lm_head分组），在RTX 5090上实测13种W8A8变体，最佳方案（FFN 5+5 + lm_head）实现11.0%延迟降低且质量损失可忽略（Top-1一致率98.90%，困惑度退化+0.85%）；FFN all26 + lm_head方案在可接受质量损失下可达19.1%加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的均匀层量化方法（如GPTQ、AWQ）耗时耗资源，而MixLLM、TorchAO等分配方法缺乏已验证的加速效果。作者旨在提出一种既考虑质量约束又能实际加速的分层比特分配方案，避免均匀量化的低效，填补按层敏感性分配精度且能实证加速的研究空白。

Method: 将层比特分配建模为约束优化问题（在退化预算内最大化性能/降低延迟）；采用SA-PTQ层敏感性分析，在TensorRT-LLM中以激活直通模式应用；按分组（5+5、10+10、all26）逐块确定每层精度，区分FFN、Attention与lm_head对整体加速的贡献；在RTX 5090上实测13种W8A8变体的时钟速度；针对TensorRT-LLM手动实现SmoothQuant（因导出失败且lm_head不可用）。

Result: FFN和lm_head中量化/反量化的时间开销被整数运算补偿，但短上下文时Attention的额外量化步骤反而拖慢执行。最佳方案（FFN 5+5 + lm_head）降低11.0%延迟，Top-1一致率98.90%，困惑度退化仅+0.85%；FFN all26 + lm_head在可接受质量损失下实现最高19.1%加速。

Conclusion: 分层比特分配在质量约束内可显著加速Gemma-3-1B推理；FFN和lm_head适合INT8量化加速，而Attention在短上下文下不适合额外量化。未来方向包括INT8融合注意力核、KV缓存量化、改用FP8、以及类似FFN的注意力部分量化。

Abstract: This paper proposes a layer bit allocation method for Gemma-3-1B, formulating the problem as performance maximization (latency decrease) given a degradation budget constraint (allowable level of generation quality loss). This approach is different from time- and resource-consuming uniform layer quantization methods that are used in the literature (like GPTQ or AWQ) or allocation methods without proven performance-accelerating effect (like MixLLM or TorchAO). The layer sensitivity profile resulting from our prior work SA-PTQ is applied using the activation pass-through mode inside TensorRT-LLM. For each layer precision is determined individually in blocks, according to a grouping introduced in the prior step (5+5, 10+10, all26), differentiating the contribution of FFN, Attention, and lm_head to the overall speedup. The clock speed was measured for 13 W8A8 variants on an RTX 5090. We find that for FFN and lm_head the time cost of quantization/dequantization is compensated for by the use of integer arithmetic, while for short context lengths, the opposite holds true for Attention: an additional step of quantization slows execution down. We propose a manual implementation of SmoothQuant for TensorRT-LLM which was necessary due to export failures, unavailable for lm_head. The best solution found under joint consideration of all three criteria with minimal degradation was FFN 5+5 with lm_head, providing an 11.0% reduction in latency with negligible quality loss (98.90% Top-1 agreement, +0.85% perplexity degradation). With acceptable quality loss for FFN all26 + lm_head, a speedup up to 19.1% was found possible. We suggest further optimizations: fused attention kernels in INT8, KV-cache quantization, using FP8 instead of INT8 and partial Attention quantization analogous to FFN.

</details>


### [267] [Exact Risk Ratios for Weighted Data Selection in Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28007)
*Guangjian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hanneke等人在COLT 2025提出的开放问题：选择器从有限数据集D⊆R^d×R中挑选至多n个带非负权重的样本，交给最小范数ERM。定义了F_w(d,n)为返回预测器在所有D上的损失与最优损失的最坏情形比率。在开放区间d<n<2d内，作者确定了多个情况下的精确值：对所有d证明F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d，对F_w(3,4)=5/3和F_w(4,5)=2给出证明，并给出了下界1+Γ_{d,k}（基于平衡划分的调和量），该下界在具有正交电路块结构的白化梯度系统数据集类上是精确极小极大值。所有三个精确值均匹配1+Γ_{d,k}，作者猜想在整个开放区间上等式成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hanneke等人在COLT 2025中提出了关于选择器与最小范数ERM组合的最坏情形损失比率的开放问题。他们已确定F_w(d,n)=∞（n<d）、F_w(d,d)=d+1以及F_w(d,n)=1（n≥2d），但中间区域d<n<2d的精确值未知。本文旨在填补这一空白，确定开放区间内的精确值。

Method: 核心方法包括：1) 对每个d证明F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d（验证原笔记中未给出证明的断言）；2) 通过分类和结构约简，证明F_w(3,4)=5/3和F_w(4,5)=2（小正基在R^3和R^4中的分类）；3) 对中间预算n=d+k，建立下界1+Γ_{d,k}，其中Γ_{d,k}是基于平衡划分的显式调和量；4) 证明该下界在具有正交电路块结构的白化梯度系统数据集类上是精确的；5) 证明上界的关键是一个共同几何脊柱：损失梯度正张成配置的刚性定理、小正基的结构约简以及将符号锥几何转化为五点选择的维数无关极值基论证；6) 给出反例说明若干较短路径失败，并提供构造性多项式时间选择算法。

Result: 主要结果：对所有d成立F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d；F_w(3,4)=5/3；F_w(4,5)=2（覆盖了端点公式未涵盖的两个最小单元）；对中间预算n=d+k，证明下界F_w(d,d+k)≥1+Γ_{d,k}，并在具有正交电路块结构的数据集类上证明该下界精确。所有三个精确值均匹配1+Γ_{d,k}。

Conclusion: 作者对所有d和三个小维数情况给出了开放问题的精确答案，建立了统一的上界证明框架（基于梯度正张成的刚性定理、小正基分类和符号锥极值论证），并猜想等式F_w(d,n)=1+Γ_{d,k}在整个开放区间d<n<2d上成立。同时提供反例说明某些自然尝试的失败，并为所有已验证情况给出多项式时间算法。

Abstract: Hanneke, Moran, Shlimovich and Yehudayoff (COLT 2025) posed the following open problem. A selector sees a finite dataset $D \subseteq \mathbb{R}^d \times \mathbb{R}$, picks at most $n$ examples together with nonnegative weights, and hands the weighted least squares objective to the minimum-norm ERM. Writing $F_w(d,n)$ for the worst-case ratio between the loss of the returned predictor on all of $D$ and the optimal loss, they proved $F_w(d,n)=\infty$ for $n<d$, $F_w(d,d)=d+1$ and $F_w(d,n)=1$ for $n \ge 2d$, and asked for the value in the open regime $d<n<2d$. We determine this value in several cases. For every $d$ we prove $F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d$, which confirms a claim stated without proof in the original note. We further prove $F_w(3,4)=5/3$ and $F_w(4,5)=2$, the two smallest cells not covered by the endpoint formula.
