The new matrix admits a $(2,0)$-partition into ten blocks, so Construction $\mathrm{QM}_2^2$ propagates it to exhaustively verified codes of lengths $815$ and $1631$ at $r=18$ and $r=20$, and to the family $n=51\cdot 2^{r/2-5}-1$ of asymptotic density $2601/2048$. The matrices, verifiers, and source are at https://github.com/wustep/maths, pin problems/covering/share/2026-08-24/ at commit 736a38f.

</details>


### [301] [Selective Interference Suppression of Siamese-Net in Heterogeneous Interference Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27635)
*Arkadeep Sinha,Shubham Paul,R. Manivasakan,Nambi Seshadri,R. David Koilpillai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个N用户实高斯干扰信道下的端到端学习短块码，重点关注4用户非均匀干扰配置（一对主干扰对和若干弱干扰对），通过Siamese式耦合训练实现选择性干扰抑制：主导干扰对编码本近乎正交，而弱耦合对保持对齐以保留编码增益，并通过隐空间相似性统计量量化该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构成对干扰强度的多用户干扰信道中，传统方法往往追求全局正交性以消除干扰，但这会牺牲编码增益。作者试图探究Siamese式耦合训练是否能够根据实际干扰强度自适应地选择性编码（解码），在正交性与编码增益之间达到最优折中，而非盲目施加全局正交约束。

Method: 采用SiameseNet进行端到端学习短块码，构建一个4用户非均匀干扰配置：一个主导干扰对(1,2)（强度Λ）和一个弱耦合背景对（强度λ）。训练后通过隐空间跨用户相似性统计量（最坏情况相干性度量、平均相似性度量等）量化编码本几何结构，并将这些几何特征与不等干扰下的BLER鲁棒性进行关联。

Result: 观察到选择性干扰抑制现象：学习到的编码本主要对主导干扰对近乎正交，而弱耦合用户对保持对齐以保留编码增益。几何签名（相干性和相似性度量）与BLER性能之间存在明确关联，表明SiameseNet能够选择性地抑制不同干扰用户对的干扰。

Conclusion: SiameseNet能够根据实际干扰强度自适应地选择性抑制干扰，在正交性（抗干扰）与编码增益（容量利用）之间实现最优权衡，而非对所有用户对强制全局正交，从而在不等干扰信道中获得更好的性能。

Abstract: We study an end-to-end learnt short-block codes for a $N$-user real Gaussian interference channel with heterogeneous pairwise interference strengths, while keeping single-user decoding at every receiver. In this paper, we study the case wherein only a few dominant interferers exist and investigate whether Siamese-style coupled training can adapt selectively to encode (\& decode) to ensure optimal performance corresponding to best tradeoff between orthogonality and coding gain or it enforces unnecessary global orthogonality oblivious of the reality. Our work focuses on a 4-user unequal-interference configuration with one dominant pair $(1,2)$ (of strength $Λ$) and a weak pair (with weak background coupling ($λ$)), through which we demonstrate a selective interference suppression phenomenon where the learned codebooks become near-orthogonal primarily for the dominant pair, while weakly coupled pairs retain alignment needed for coding gain. We quantify this behaviour using latent-space cross-user similarity statistics (worst-case coherence measure, average similarity measure, etc) and connect these geometric signatures to the observed BLER robustness under unequal interference. It seems that the SiameseNet selectively suppresses the interferences from various interferring user pairs to yield optimal tradeoff between coding gain and BLER dictated by orthogonality.

</details>


### [302] [Low-Altitude Fluid Antenna Network with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27909)
*Tong Zhang,Yanfei Su,Shuai Wang,Wanli Ni,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种电磁数字孪生（EM-DT）辅助的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，用于低空无线网络（LAWNs）中流形天线（FA）的快速高性能重构，并通过两阶段迁移学习填补仿真与真实之间的差距。案例研究表明，联合FA位置与波束成形优化相比固定位置基线可将系统总速率提升118.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络整合地面与空中平台，为无人机和eVTOL提供通信、感知与定位服务，但动态的空-地和空-空信道、突发遮挡以及异构干扰严重阻碍了目标的实现。流形天线作为先进的MIMO技术可通过重构天线位置解锁额外的空间自由度来克服这些挑战，然而如何实现快速且高性能的FA重构是一大难题。

Method: 本文提出EM-DT辅助的MARL框架，利用电磁数字孪生构建贴近真实的高保真电磁环境，通过多智能体强化学习实现FA阵列位置与波束成形的联合优化。为填补仿真到真实的差距，引入两阶段迁移学习框架，先在仿真环境预训练，再迁移到真实场景进行微调。

Result: 案例研究显示，联合FA位置与波束成形优化相比固定位置基线可将系统总速率提升118.5%，该增益来源于FA阵列在毫秒级时间尺度上的动态重构以及朝向具有移动性的空中用户的自适应波束导向。

Conclusion: 通过EM-DT辅助的MARL框架和两阶段迁移学习，能够实现低空FA网络中的快速高性能FA重构，显著提升系统性能，为低空FA网络的落地实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs) integrate terrestrial and aerial platforms to provide ubiquitous communication, sensing, and localization services for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) aircraft. However, dynamic air-ground and air-air channels, abrupt blockages, and heterogeneous interference hinder the realization of this goal. Nevertheless, fluid antenna (FA), a cutting-edge multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technique, overcomes these challenges by reconfiguring antenna positions to unlock additional spatial degrees-of-freedom. In this paper, towards bringing low-altitude FA networks into reality, we study the fast and high-performance FA reconfiguration for low-altitude FA networks with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Specifically, we present an electromagnetic digital twin (EM-DT)-assisted MARL framework. To fill the sim-to-real gap, we introduce a two-stage transfer learning framework. Our case study shows that joint FA positions and beamforming optimization can enhance the system sum-rate by 118.5%, compared to the fixed position baseline. This gain comes from the dynamic millisecond timescale reconfiguration of FA arrays and the adaptive steering of beams toward aerial users with mobility.

</details>


### [303] [Finite Sample Bounds for Composite Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28068)
*El{í}as Vera-Sig{ü}enza,Amedeo Roberto Esposito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限样本下非对称误差约束的复合二元假设检验，利用Rényi散度导出了最优第二类误差的显式可达性与逆界。当第一类误差以指数速率衰减时，界揭示了相变现象并给出其上的强逆。在复合问题中，相变阈值由零假设类与备择假设类上的联合KL投影决定。可达成性通过联合Rényi投影实现，其对数似然比定义了单一检验，可对两类假设实现均匀误差控制。对于有限字母表上具有全支撑的紧凸类，论文确定了相变两侧的精确误差指数，并证明可达指数在唯一Rényi阶处取得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合假设检验是统计推断的核心问题，但在有限样本下导出精确误差界的挑战长期存在。以往结果多依赖最小有利假设对的假设，这在复合场景中未必成立。作者旨在提供不依赖最小有利假设框架的通用有限样本界，并揭示误差指数随样本量变化的相变结构。

Method: 利用Rényi散度构建联合Rényi投影（joint Rényi projection），其对数似然比定义了单一检验统计量，可在两类假设上实现均匀误差控制。推导可达成性与逆界，识别相变阈值（联合KL投影），并在紧凸类全支撑有限字母表假设下以唯一Rényi阶精确求解相变两侧的误差指数。同时将框架推广到固定第一类误差情形以恢复Chernoff-Stein指数，并导出有限样本可达成性的多项式细化。

Result: 确定了有限样本复合检验的最优第二类误差上下界，识别出由联合KL投影刻画的相变阈值。在相变之上建立了强逆；在紧凸类有限字母表条件下，给出了相变两侧的精确误差指数，且可达指数在唯一Rényi阶处取得。框架还恢复了复合Chernoff-Stein指数，并给出了有限样本可达成性的多项式阶细化。进一步给出了投影对在有限样本下是最小有利的充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文建立了复合二元假设检验在非对称误差约束下有限样本误差指数的完整刻画，通过Rényi散度与联合投影统一了可达成性与强逆结果，不仅推广了经典Chernoff-Stein理论到复合设定，还揭示了有限样本下的相变结构与多项式细化效应，为复合假设检验提供了系统性的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate composite binary hypothesis testing in the finite sample regime under asymmetric error constraints. Using Rényi divergences, we derive explicit achievability and converse bounds for the optimal Type II error. When the Type I error is constrained to decay exponentially with sample size, the bounds identify a phase transition and yield a strong converse above it. In the composite problem, the phase transition threshold is given by the joint KL projection over the alternative and null classes. Achievability is obtained through a joint Rényi projection whose log likelihood ratio defines a single test with uniform error control over both hypothesis classes, without requiring the projected pair to be least favourable. For compact convex classes with full support on a finite alphabet, we determine the exact error exponents on both sides of the transition and show that the achievable exponent is attained at a unique Rényi order. The same framework recovers the fixed Type I composite Chernoff--Stein exponent and yields a polynomial refinement of the finite sample achievability result. We further identify conditions under which the projected pair is least favourable at finite sample size.

</details>


### [304] [ODMA-based MIMO Massive Unsourced Random Access with Soft-Output Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28085)
*Tianya Li,Xiaoran Zhang,Nan Hu,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang,Xiang-Gen Xia,Chengshan Xiao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对MIMO大规模无源随机接入（URA）系统，设计了基于软输出（SO）极化码的开关分多址（ODMA）传输方案。提出三段落非耦合编码架构、基于消息传递的层级模式检测算法，以及联合模式检测与数据译码的迭代算法，利用极化码软输出信息和多层次检测框架显著提升系统性能并降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模无源随机接入（URA）系统中，传统ODMA方案在编码速率和传输开销之间存在瓶颈。如何在无需额外开销的前提下降低数据段编码速率、提升系统整体性能，同时兼顾模式检测精度与译码性能，是本文需要解决的核心问题。

Method: 1) 提出三段式（pilot-uncoupled）编码方案，在ODMA框架下降低数据段编码速率而不增加传输开销；2) 构建层级模式检测框架：先通过相关运算获得粗粒度候选模式集，再基于消息传递（MP）算法迭代估计模式的后续概率，最后通过最大后验（MAP）估计获得精确检测结果；3) 利用极化译码器的逐位软输出（SO）信息，实现模式检测与数据译码的联合迭代优化；4) 基于SCL极化译码器的逐位软输出信息，开发MP迭代译码算法以增强译码性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的方案实现了较强的鲁棒性，能够同时利用uncoupled-ODMA框架的传输增益、极化码在短块长下的编码增益，以及由软输出信息驱动的迭代译码增益，同时通过层级检测框架显著降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ODMA传输方案通过三段式编码架构与层级检测框架的有效结合，在MIMO大规模URA系统中实现了性能与复杂度的良好折中，验证了软输出极化码在无源随机接入场景中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: This paper investigates the design of the on-off division multiple access (ODMA) transmission scheme for multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) massive unsourced random access (URA) systems with soft-output (SO) polar codes. First, a three-segment pilot-uncoupled coding scheme is introduced under the ODMA framework, which reduces the coding rate of the data segment without increasing the transmission overhead, improving the overall system performance. Building upon this architecture, a hierarchical pattern detection framework is developed. Specifically, a coarse-grained candidate set of transmission patterns is first identified through correlation operations. Based on this, a message-passing (MP)-based pattern detection algorithm is developed to iteratively estimate the posterior probabilities of transmission patterns, followed by the \textit{maximum a posteriori} (MAP) estimation to obtain the precise pattern detection result. Furthermore, a joint pattern detection and data decoding algorithm based on the bit-wise SO information of polar decoder is investigated, where the posterior probability information provided by the polar decoder is exploited to refine the pattern detection and contribute to an improved accuracy. In addition, by leveraging bit-wise SO information of the successive cancellation list polar decoder, an MP-based iterative decoding algorithm is developed to significantly enhance the decoding performance. The proposed scheme simultaneously exploits the transmission gain of uncoupled-ODMA framework, the coding gain of polar codes in the short-blocklength regime, and the iterative decoding gain enabled by SO information, while the computational complexity is significantly reduced through the hierarchical detection framework. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves strong robustness ...

</details>


### [305] [Ada-TokenCom: Rate-Adaptive Token Communications via Large-Model-Driven Token Compression and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28086)
*Zijun Zhang,Li Qiao,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Zhen Gao,Mehdi Bennis,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出Ada-TokenCom，一种基于大型自回归模型的速率自适应Token通信框架，通过将下一token预测与算术编码结合，实现token级别的超低码率语义通信，并利用Lyapunov优化动态调整压缩率与调制编码方案，在仿真中优于数字与深度联合信源信道编码基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TokenCom作为一种以token为统一通信与计算单元的新范式，为多模态语义与目标导向传输提供了高效途径。然而，现有方法缺乏对速率自适应的支持，难以在时变网络条件下实现超低码率的高效语义通信，亟需一个能够动态调整传输速率的TokenCom框架。

Method: 论文提出混合重建/生成方案：发射端利用预训练自回归大模型编码并传输出token序列前部的高信息量token，接收端使用相同模型预测剩余token。同时设计基于Lyapunov的算法，动态联合优化源压缩率与调制编码方案（MCS），以适应时变网络条件。

Result: 仿真结果表明，Ada-TokenCom在性能上优于数字语义通信基线和深度联合信源信道编码（deep JSCC）语义通信基线。

Conclusion: Ada-TokenCom证明了将大型自回归模型的token预测能力与算术编码及Lyapunov速率控制相结合的可行性，为token级语义通信提供了速率自适应的新路径，且在超低码率下表现优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Token Communications (TokenCom) has recently emerged as a new paradigm in which tokens serve as unified units for communication and computation, enabling efficient multimodal semantic and goal-oriented transmission. In this paper, we develop Ada-TokenCom, a rate-adaptive TokenCom framework based on large autoregressive models, which integrates next-token prediction with arithmetic coding to achieve ultra-low bitrate semantic communication at the token level. We propose a mixed reconstruction/generation scheme, where the transmitter encodes and transmits the highly informative tokens at the beginning of the token sequence leveraging a pre-trained autoregressive large model, while the receiver uses an identical model to predict the rest. Moreover, we design a Lyapunov-based algorithm to dynamically optimize both the source compression rate and the modulation and coding scheme, adapting to time-varying network conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed Ada-TokenCom framework outperforms both digital and deep joint source-channel coding-based semantic communication baselines.

</details>


### [306] [Fine Difference Structure and Prime-Power Depth of Bent Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28133)
*Zhaorui Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了p元bent划分中每个单元的大小必然相等，且划分单元数K必为p的幂次，并进一步给出K的指数不超过n/2的全局界。证明通过有限平均计数恒等式完成，并在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于p元bent划分的深度K是否为p的幂次这一开放问题，仅在正则性或单元对称性等额外假设下得到肯定结论。论文的目标是证明无条件的更强结论，回答这一公开问题。

Method: 证明的关键在于证明一个无条件的更强命题：对每个非零平移h，恰好有p^n/K个点在该平移下仍留在同一细格单元中。由此证明细格单元构成划分差分族，细标签映射是零差分平衡的。证明工具是对平衡细化取精确的有限平均，并通过Leaning 4进行计数恒等式的形式化与内核验证。

Result: 证明了K整除p^n，从而K=p^t，且非空单元给出1≤t<n；在偶数维情形下，经典单元尺寸定理给出K整除p^{n/2}，结合已知的奇维三元三纤维参数限制，得到全局上界t≤⌊n/2⌋。

Conclusion: 论文以无条件的方式解决了bent划分深度为p的幂次的问题，并给出了更紧的全局维数界。所有主要计数恒等式及推论均在Lean 4证明助手中完成了形式化验证，确保了证明的可靠性。

Abstract: A $p$-ary bent partition of $\mathbb{F}_p^n$ is a partition into $K$ nonempty cells such that every balanced assignment of its cells to $\mathbb{F}_p$ produces a bent function. It was asked whether every possible depth $K$ is a power of $p$; for general $p$, previous affirmative results required regularity or cell-symmetry hypotheses. We prove the stronger unconditional statement that, for every nonzero $h$, exactly $p^n/K$ points remain in the same fine cell under translation by $h$. Thus the fine cells form a partitioned difference family and the fine label map is zero-difference balanced. Consequently $K\mid p^n$, so $K=p^t$; nonempty cells further give $1\le t<n$. In even dimension, the classical cell-size theorem yields $K\mid p^{n/2}$. Together with the known odd-dimensional ternary three-fibre parameter restriction, this gives the global bound $t\le\lfloor n/2\rfloor$. The proof is an exact finite average over balanced coarsenings. The main counting identity and selected consequences are formalized and kernel-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [307] [Shortest self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings of linear codes over Fq+uFq](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28222)
*Junmin An,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文确定了有限环F_q+uF_q上线性码的最短自正交嵌入和最短LCD嵌入的精确长度。通过将Gram矩阵分解为F_q上对称矩阵对，将嵌入问题归结为有限域上对称矩阵与交错矩阵的同余分类问题，得到了最短自正交嵌入长度的完整公式（在偶特征和奇特征下各有两种不同情形），并利用Witt理论构造所有最短自正交嵌入；同时给出了最短LCD嵌入在生成矩阵拼接指定尺寸的可逆/任意矩阵层面上的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自正交码和LCD码在编码理论中具有重要地位（如量子纠错码和密码学应用），而将一个线性码嵌入到自正交码或LCD码中是一个基础性问题。此前对有限域上的最短嵌入长度已有研究，但有限环F_q+uF_q上的情形尚未被系统解决，需要确定最短嵌入的精确长度并给出构造方法。

Method: 将F_q+uF_q上的Gram矩阵分解为F_q上的一对对称矩阵，从而把嵌入问题转化为有限域上对称矩阵和交错矩阵的同余分类问题；利用Witt理论对所有最短自正交嵌入进行构造；对LCD嵌入，通过向生成矩阵追加指定尺寸的可逆矩阵或任意矩阵给出完整刻画。

Result: 获得了最短自正交嵌入长度的完整公式，在偶特征和奇特征下各出现两种不同情形；证明了F_q+uF_q上具有非零自由秩的自正交码均可视为另一码的最短自正交嵌入；建立了最短LCD嵌入的完整刻画；给出了使所考虑码具有最大最小距离的自正交和LCD嵌入实例，部分Gray像为F_q上的最优码。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了F_q+uF_q上线性码的最短自正交嵌入与最短LCD嵌入的精确长度问题，不仅在理论上通过同余分类和Witt理论给出了完备的刻画与构造，还通过实例展示了所得嵌入在最小距离方面的优化性质，其中部分Gray像达到最优码。

Abstract: This paper determines the exact lengths of shortest self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings of linear codes over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$. By decomposing Gram matrices over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$ into pairs of symmetric matrices over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$, the embedding problems are reduced to the congruence classification of symmetric and alternate matrices over finite fields. Complete formulas for the shortest self-orthogonal embedding length are obtained, with two distinct cases arising in both even and odd characteristic. We also show that every self-orthogonal code over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$ with nonzero free rank can be viewed as a shortest self-orthogonal embedding of another code. We use Witt theory to construct all shortest self-orthogonal embeddings. A complete characterization of shortest LCD embeddings is also established in terms of invertible and arbitrary matrices of prescribed sizes appended to a generator matrix. Examples of self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings with the largest minimum distance for the code considered are also presented, some of whose Gray images are optimal codes over $\mathbb{F}_q$.

</details>


### [308] [Significance-Driven Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28441)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Sirin Chakraborty,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于显著性驱动的跨层语义通信设计框架，在物理层引入元学习变分信息瓶颈（Meta-VIB）语义收发机，在MAC层将信道分配建模为多动作可重启多臂老虎机（MA-RMAB）并采用Q-Maximization算法，以最大化语义频谱效率，在真实行人安全数据集上实现了高达1000倍的语义频谱效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信研究通常将物理层和MAC层分开设计，缺乏对每个样本任务价值（数据显著性）的量化度量，导致资源分配无法反映数据在不同任务中的真实重要程度，且动态信道条件和变化符号预算下的在线重训练开销大。

Method: 基于统计决策理论引入信息论意义上的每样本数据显著性度量；在物理层设计Meta-VIB语义收发机，利用元学习超网络实现高维观测到语义显著潜变量的压缩，无需在线重训练即可瞬时适应动态信道和符号预算；在MAC层将信道分配建模为MA-RMAB，采用Q-Maximization算法依据语义信息价值动态分配信道资源，联合优化物理层编解码推断与MAC层资源分配。

Result: 在真实行人安全数据集上的实验表明，相比基线方法，联合设计方案在平均SNR为0 dB时达到高达1000倍的语义频谱效率增益，在平均SNR为5 dB时达到40倍的增益。

Conclusion: 跨层联合优化语义编码与资源分配是显著提升语义频谱效率的有效途径，深度学习与强化学习相结合的方法能够在动态无线环境中实现高效、自适应的语义通信。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a significance-driven cross- layer semantic communication design problem. Based on sta- tistical decision theory, we introduce an information-theoretic measure of per-sample data significance that quantifies the task-specific value of each individual observation. Using this metric, we formulate a cross-layer optimization problem that simultaneously optimizes (i) physical-layer semantic encoding and inference and (ii) MAC-layer resource allocation, with the objective of maximizing semantic spectrum efficiency, defined as the semantic value delivered per unit bandwidth per unit time. At the physical layer, we develop Meta-Learning Variational Information Bottleneck (Meta-VIB), a new semantic transceiver that employs a meta-learned hypernetwork to compress high- dimensional observations into semantically significant latents, enabling instantaneous adaptation to dynamic channel conditions and varying symbol budgets without online retraining. At the MAC layer, we model channel allocation as a Multi-Action Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (MA-RMAB) and adopt the Q- Maximization algorithm, which dynamically allocates channel resources to sensors based on their semantic value of information. Experimental results on a real-world pedestrian safety dataset demonstrate that our joint design achieves substantial gains in semantic spectrum efficiency over baselines, reaching up to 1000 times gain at an average SNR of 0 dB and 40 times gain at an average SNR of 5 dB.

</details>


### [309] [Distributed Cross-Layer Optimization for Covert Multi-Hop, Multi-Modal Networks: Exponentially Fast Convergence and Robust Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28469)
*Sirin Chakraborty,Andrea Panebianco,Yuchen Tian,Kevin S Chan,Fikadu Dagefu,Yin Sun,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了第一个面向隐蔽多跳、多模态无线网络的分布式跨层算法，联合优化拥塞控制、路由、调度和功率控制。通过构造log-DEP最紧的凹下界将非凹的隐蔽约束转化为凸问题，并采用并行近端ADMM算法求解，证明了全局Q线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐蔽多跳多模态无线网络中，对抗性监视者（Willies）通过能量检测监控无线电模态，这使得检测错误概率（DEP）成为关键的隐蔽性指标。然而DEP在发射功率上一般是非凹的，使得基于DEP的隐蔽网络优化非常困难，缺乏有效的分布式跨层求解算法。

Method: 1) 构造log-DEP最紧的凹下界，将非凸的DEP约束转换为保守凸问题，统一处理硬隐蔽约束与隐蔽性-效用最大化；2) 提出并行近端交替方向乘子法（PP-ADMM）求解该跨层问题；3) 在标准正则性条件下证明算法全局Q线性收敛到最优解集。

Result: 数值结果验证了算法的线性收敛特性，并在信道衰落和Willie移动场景下表现出鲁棒的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了隐蔽多跳多模态网络的分布式跨层优化，所提PP-ADMM算法兼具理论收敛保证与实际鲁棒性，为隐蔽网络资源分配提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper develops the first distributed cross-layer algorithm for joint congestion control, routing, scheduling, and power control in covert multi-hop, multi-modal wireless networks, where adversarial wardens (Willies) monitor radio modalities via energy detection. The Detection Error Probability (DEP), the probability that a Willie fails to reliably detect ongoing transmissions, is generally non-concave in the transmit powers, making DEP-based covert network optimization challenging. We resolve this by constructing the tightest concave lower bound on the log-DEP, yielding a conservative convex problem that guarantees satisfaction of the original DEP constraints and unifies hard covertness constraints and covertness-utility maximization in a single problem. We develop a Parallel Proximal Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (PP-ADMM) algorithm for the resulting cross-layer problem and prove global Q-linear convergence, i.e., exponentially fast convergence, to the set of optimal solutions under standard regularity conditions. Numerical results confirm linear convergence and demonstrate robust tracking performance under channel fading and Willie mobility.

</details>


### [310] [A Complete Characterization of Tensorizable $f$-divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28556)
*Rodrigo Cruz,Flavio P. Calmon,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文细化并推广了f-散度的张量化（tensorization）形式体系，证明任何张量化公式都具有由单一参数刻画的多仿射形式，并完整刻画了在该张量化概念下所有可张量化的f-散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然f-散度家族非常广泛，但实际应用中仅少数几个f-散度（如KL散度、χ²-散度和平方Hellinger距离）被广泛使用，一个重要原因是它们满足在乘积测度下的简单复合公式（即张量化性质）。此前文献中已有张量化的初步形式体系，但该文旨在进一步细化这一形式体系，以更精确地理解张量化性质的结构。

Method: 作者对已有文献中的张量化形式体系进行了细化，在此基础上推导出任何可能的张量化公式必须具有多仿射（multi-affine）形式，并由单一参数唯一刻画。利用这一结构结果，他们系统性地识别并刻画了在所采用的张量化定义下所有满足张量化性质的f-散度。

Result: 证明了所有张量化公式均具有由单个参数决定的多仿射形式，并给出了在给定张量化概念下所有可张量化f-散度的完整分类。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解f-散度家族中哪些成员具有张量化性质提供了完整的理论刻画，解释了为何KL散度、χ²-散度和平方Hellinger距离等少数散度在统计学和信息论中被特殊重视，并为未来在张量化框架下构造新的可张量化散度提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Csiszar's formulation of the $f$-divergence introduced a vast family of functionals for quantifying dissimilarity between probability distributions. However, many applications in statistics and information theory rely only on a few $f$-divergences, such as the Kullback-Leibler divergence, the $χ^2$-divergence, and the squared Hellinger distance. These divergences are especially useful because they admit simple compositional formulas under product measures, a property sometimes referred to as tensorization. In this work, we refine a formalism of tensorization previously introduced in the literature. Then, we show that any possible tensorization formula has a multi-affine form characterized by a single parameter, and identify all tensorizable $f$-divergences under our adopted notion of tensorization.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [Visual Cue Interactions in AR-Guided Needle Insertion: A Prostate Biopsy-Inspired Phantom Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27620)
*Xinrui Zou,Mingxu Liu,Thomas T. Jones,Braden Millan,Sandeep Gurram,Peter A. Pinto,Raisa Z. Freidlin,Alejandro Martin-Gomez*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文研究经皮穿刺手术中增强现实（AR）的三种可视化技术（局部聚焦-上下文窗口、颜色邻近编码、显式轨迹叠加）对针具对准的影响。用户在26名受试者（含7名临床专家）的模拟前列腺活检实验中评估发现，提示效果取决于周围视觉线索配置和用户经验水平：新手受益于显式轨迹叠加，专家则受益于聚焦-上下文窗口和颜色编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经皮穿刺手术中，临床医生依赖超声图像而不能直接看到内部解剖结构，操作工具方向十分困难且认知负荷高。AR有望通过在视野中提供视觉信息来辅助，但简单地提供不包含有意义视觉线索的信息反而会复杂化深度感知和空间理解。因此需要系统评估不同可视化技术。

Method: 与医学专家进行设计会话后引入三种可视化技术（局部聚焦-上下文窗口、基于颜色的邻近编码、显式轨迹叠加），并在前列腺活检启发式体模上进行用户研究（n=26，其中临床专家7人）进行评估。

Result: 提示效果取决于周围线索配置和用户经验。对新手而言，显式轨迹叠加提高了瞄准精度并减少了撤退行为，但单独呈现时完成时间变慢；对专家而言，聚焦-上下文窗口减少了完成时间和撤退事件，颜色邻近覆盖提高了完成时间。颜色线索在主观上常被认为有帮助，即便其对精度的效果不一致。

Conclusion: 经皮介入的AR引导策略应考虑用户经验和视觉上下文，不同可视化技术对不同经验水平用户的效果存在显著差异，需要个性化或自适应设计。

Abstract: Despite the apparent simplicity of the motor action involved during percutaneous needle procedures, manipulating the tool's direction becomes challenging when clinicians cannot directly visualize internal anatomy and must rely on ultrasound images, which increase cognitive demand. Augmented reality (AR) offers the promise to assist with these tasks by providing pertinent visual information in the clinician's field of view. However, simply providing visual information that ignores meaningful visual cues can complicate depth perception and spatial understanding. In this work, we introduce and evaluate three visualization techniques for needle alignment developed during design sessions with medical experts: a localized focus-and-context window, a color-based proximity encoding, and an explicit trajectory overlay. These techniques were evaluated in a user study (n=26) including clinical experts (n=7) using a prostate-biopsy-inspired phantom. Results from this study suggest that cue effects depended on the surrounding cue configuration and user expertise. For novices, explicit trajectory overlay improved targeting accuracy and reduced retreat behavior, but its effect on completion time varied across cue configurations, with slower performance when the overlay was presented alone. For experts, the focus-and-context window reduced completion time and retreat events, while color-based proximity overlay improved completion time. Subjectively, color cues were often perceived as helpful even when their effects on accuracy were not consistent. These results suggest that AR guidance strategies for percutaneous interventions should consider user expertise and visual context.

</details>


### [312] [The Role of Mixed and Augmented Reality in Medical Visualization: Literature Review and A Context-Aware Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27644)
*Xinrui Zou,Mingxu Liu,Hongchao Shu,Ruixing Liang,Mathias Unberath,Alejandro Martin-Gomez*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向医学AR/MR可视化的结构化分类法，基于四个核心设计组件（图像模态、数据维度、显示技术和临床应用）以及两个常被忽视的关键维度（可视化锚定和感知意识），为不同临床场景中选择合适的可视化技术提供系统化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学成像虽能提供非侵入式的内部解剖可视化，但传统二维显示方式难以充分呈现三维解剖结构。AR/MR技术虽提供了更直观的空间交互范式，但若缺乏仔细的设计考量贸然实施，可能导致感知不一致，影响其可用性和有效性。因此需要系统化的分类框架来指导医学AR/MR可视化设计。

Method: 提出一个结构化的医学AR/MR可视化策略分类法，将其组织为四个核心设计组件：图像模态、数据维度、显示技术和临床应用；并引入两个文献中常被忽视的关键维度：可视化锚定（虚拟内容与物理世界之间的空间关系）和感知意识（支持空间解释的视觉线索使用）。

Result: 形成了一个综合性的分类法框架，涵盖六个维度（四个核心组件加两个补充维度），为医学应用中可视化技术的选择提供了详细且系统的指导框架。

Conclusion: 该分类法通过全面的维度体系弥补了现有文献中可视化锚定和感知意识两个维度的缺失，为医学AR/MR可视化技术的设计与选择提供了系统化的参考框架，有助于提升可视化在不同临床应用中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: The discovery and evolution of medical imaging technologies have enabled non-invasive visualization of internal anatomy that has be- come essential for supporting diagnosis, monitoring, and treatment. However, because medical imaging relies on complex physical processes and contrast mechanisms for image formation, imaging alone is not sufficient to enable humans to leverage the resulting in- formation fully. In addition, traditional methods to visualize the resulting information use two-dimensional displays to present three-dimensional anatomical structures. The introduction of Augmented and Mixed Reality (AR/MR) technologies offers an opportunity to provide valuable paradigms for medical imaging visualization, allowing users to observe, explore, and interact with anatomical infor- mation in more spatially intuitive ways. However, naive implementation without careful design considerations can lead to perceptual inconsistencies, potentially compromising utility and effectiveness. In this paper, we present a structured taxonomy of medical AR/MR visualization strategies aimed at providing clearer insight into how visualization design varies across clinical use cases. The taxonomy organizes techniques based on four core design components: image modality, data dimensionality, display technology, and clinical application. In addition, we introduce two critical dimensions that are often overlooked in the literature: visualization anchoring (the spatial relationship between virtual content and the physical world), and perceptual awareness (the use of visual cues to support spatial interpretation). Together, these components form a comprehensive taxonomy, offering a detailed framework for selecting appropriate visualization techniques in medical applications.

</details>


### [313] [What Will This Copper Look Like Later? Forecasting Surface Appearance and Rendering It as a PBR Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28102)
*Teejuta Sriwaranon,Borworntat Dendumrongkul,Tanapat Chamted,Pizzanu Kanongchaiyos*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向铜表面氧化的数字设计预测流水线：给定固定相机观测，系统预测目标在10个加速时间单位后的外观，并将其转换为渲染器所需的反照率、法线、粗糙度和金属度贴图。在跨样本（未见样本）协议下，预训练模型均不如"复制最后一帧"，唯一可迁移的是无参数的封闭式全局颜色外推法，在t+10时分别提升16.7%和55.5%。因此流水线对未见样本采用封闭式预测器，对已观测样本采用学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字设计需要预测金属表面在其氧化后期将呈现的外观，而铜是一种典型会随氧化改变外观的金属材料，但目前缺乏可靠、可迁移的预测方法。

Method: 采用严格的单样本留出协议（一个样本用于训练和选模，另一整段不同天、不同条件的录制作测试）评估多种预测器：包括具有单调氧化状态的学习型时空模型、三种其他训练架构、以及无参数的封闭式全局颜色外推法。同时通过光度漂移校正和移动块bootstrap进行控制实验，并分析学习到的敏感度图。

Result: 在样本内最准确的单调时空学习模型在跨样本测试中不如复制最后一帧；唯一表现优于复制最后一帧的是封闭式全局颜色外推法，提升13.4%与50.6%，且随horizon增大到+16.7%与+55.5%。光度漂移校正不影响该优势，说明并非曝光漂移所致；bootstrap将较大margin与零分离，但较小margin未达单独显著。学习到的敏感度图在训练样本上编码腐蚀起始位置，在未见样本上具有误导性，而全局颜色轨迹是样本间共享的。

Conclusion: 样本间可迁移的是全局颜色演化轨迹而非局部腐蚀位置，因此应在未见样本上使用封闭式外推器，仅在继续观察同一已见样本时才使用学习模型。代码、数据划分、协议与泄漏审计已公开。

Abstract: Digital design requires predicting how a metal surface will look later in its oxidation; this paper presents such a pipeline for copper. Given a fixed-camera observation, the system forecasts appearance 10 accelerated units ahead and converts it into the albedo, normal, roughness and metallic maps a renderer consumes. Forecasting is evaluated as an authoring tool would use it, on a copper specimen the system has not observed: an entire recording is held out, so training and checkpoint selection use one specimen and the test set is the whole of a second, recorded on a different day and condition. Under this protocol a learned spatio-temporal model with a monotone oxidation state, the most accurate forecaster within a single recording, is less accurate than copying the last observed frame on an unseen specimen, in both directions, as are three further trained architectures. The only forecaster that transfers is a closed-form global color extrapolation with no trained parameters, improving on copy-last-frame by 13.4% and 50.6%, with a margin that increases with horizon to +16.7% and +55.5% at t+10. Two controls qualify this: correcting every frame for the photometric drift measured on a non-oxidizing reference region leaves both margins intact, ruling out uncontrolled exposure as their source, and a moving-block bootstrap over the 6 independent windows each recording contains separates the larger margin from zero but leaves the smaller one not individually significant. The mechanism is measured: a learned susceptibility map encodes where corrosion begins on the training specimen and misleads on a new one, whereas the global color trajectory is what specimens share. The pipeline therefore deploys the closed-form forecaster for unseen specimens and the learned model only for continuing one already observed. Code, splits, protocol and leakage audit are released.

</details>


### [314] [Transparency Rendering in Computer-Aided Design: Methodologies, Trade-offs, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28310)
*Grigoris Tsopouridis,Ioannis Fudos*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了CAD环境中透明度渲染的现状，涵盖工业系统部署、底层算法、近似与精确技术的权衡、基准测试需求、内部关键部件强调技术，以及透明CSG物体渲染的独特挑战，并总结了开放问题与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明度渲染在CAD中对于设计审查、沟通和决策至关重要，但目前缺乏对该领域现状、算法权衡及开放问题的系统性梳理，需要一篇综述来整合分散的研究成果并指导未来方向。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统回顾了CAD环境中透明度渲染的多种技术方案，包括近似与精确透明度技术的性能-质量权衡分析、桌面与移动平台的对比、透明度技术选择标准、重要性驱动的透明度、基于轮廓的方法，以及透明CSG物体渲染的挑战与集成问题。

Result: 本文实现了对CAD透明度渲染现状的系统化总结，指出了精确与近似技术在不同平台上的权衡关系，明确了缺乏专门针对CAD可视化任务的基准和评估方法的现状，并归纳了透明CSG渲染在鲁棒性、正确性和管线集成方面的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 本文认为CAD透明度渲染领域仍存在诸多开放问题，包括需要建立CAD专用的基准与质量指标、改进透明CSG渲染的鲁棒性与正确性，以及开发面向复杂装配体的空间理解增强技术，这些方向构成了未来研究的重要议程。

Abstract: This paper surveys the state of transparency rendering in Computer-Aided Design (CAD), with a focus on both practical deployment in industrial systems and the underlying algorithms. We first review current approaches to transparency rendering in CAD environments and outline application scenarios in which accurate and performant transparency is critical for design inspection, communication, and decision-making. We then analyze the trade-offs between approximate and exact transparency techniques, comparing their performance-quality balance on desktop and mobile platforms and discussing criteria for selecting appropriate methods. The survey further identifies the need for robust benchmarks, quality metrics, and evaluation methodologies tailored to CAD-specific visualization tasks. We examine techniques for emphasizing important interior components such as importance-driven transparency, silhouette-based methods, and related approaches to support effective spatial understanding in complex assemblies. Finally, we discuss the unique challenges of rendering transparent constructive solid geometry (CSG) objects, including robustness, correctness, and integration with modern rendering pipelines. Collectively, these contributions characterize current capabilities, systematize open problems, and outline future research directions for transparency rendering in CAD visualization.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [315] [Refundable Deposits: How to Restore Cooperation in Finitely Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27536)
*Giulio Salizzoni,Domenico Mergoni Cecchelli,Edward Plumb,Maryam Kamgarpour,Galit Ashkenazi-Golan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过"保证金"（deposits）机制来扩大有限重复博弈纳什均衡集合的方法：玩家每期向中立中介支付可退还的保证金，在博弈结束时返还，偏离时没收。该机制无需玩家承诺，仅需中介承诺退款规则，可在不改变底层博弈且无玩家间转移支付的情况下，支撑比标准均衡更高效的收益配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限重复博弈存在丰富的纳什均衡集合，而有限重复博弈的均衡集通常较小且往往低效，难以实现合作。作者希望在不改变底层博弈结构、不引入玩家间转移支付的条件下，扩大有限重复博弈的均衡集合，以获得更高效的收益结果。

Method: 在每个时期，玩家可自愿向中立中介放置一笔可退还的保证金，待博弈结束时归还，若发生偏离则没收。保证金支付在每一阶段都是自愿且激励相容的，因此无需玩家作出任何承诺；唯一需要承诺的是中介在博弈开始前固定下来的退款规则。该机制不改变底层博弈，也不涉及玩家之间的转移支付。

Result: 该机制能够在有限重复博弈中维持比标准均衡更高效的收益配置。作者在囚徒困境、拥堵博弈和公共品博弈三个标准有限重复版本无法出现合作的设定中进行了验证，均成功支撑了效率更高的结果；并将该构造进一步推广到动态公共池资源问题，表明该机制可超越重复阶段博弈的框架。

Conclusion: 通过中介保证金机制，可以在无需玩家承诺或相互转移支付的前提下，有效扩大有限重复博弈的可实现均衡集合，促进合作并提升效率，且该构造可扩展到更广泛的动态博弈场景。

Abstract: While infinitely repeated games admit a rich set of Nash equilibria, finitely repeated games typically have a much smaller and often inefficient one. We show how to enlarge this set using deposits: in each period a player may place a refundable sum with a neutral intermediary, returned when the game ends and forfeited following a deviation. Paying these deposits is voluntary and incentive compatible at every stage, so no commitment by the players is assumed, the only commitment required being that of the intermediary to a refund rule fixed before play begins. The mechanism sustains payoff profiles more efficient than those of the standard equilibria, without altering the underlying game and without transfers between players. We demonstrate it on the prisoner's dilemma, a congestion game, and a public goods game, all settings where cooperation cannot emerge in the standard finitely repeated version. We also apply it to a dynamic common-pool resource, suggesting that the construction extends beyond repeated stage-games.

</details>


### [316] [Approval-Based Apportionment: Like Portioning, Approximately like Committee Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27605)
*Paul Gölz,Hannane Yaghoubizade*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究审批制分配（apportionment），证明EJR、EJR+与FJR这些比例性公理相互等价，PJR、PJR+与FPJR也相互等价；Lindahl价格性（隐含核心稳定性）等价于PAV得分的某种近似最优性；局部PAV最优的委员会是价格性的。文章还给出了从分配提升到份额划分（portioning）的公理构造，并指出分配介于委员会选举与份额划分之间，且更接近后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审批制分配作为委员会选举（每候选0或1席）与份额划分（分数席位）之间的中间模型，其公理关系尚不清楚。作者希望厘清该模型中多种比例性公理之间的蕴含关系，并通过与份额划分的形式化连接，为委员会选举中近似关系提供预测平台。

Method: 本文通过公理化分析证明EJR、EJR+、FJR的等价性以及PJR、PJR+、FPJR的等价性；建立Lindahl价格性与PAV近似最优性之间的等价关系；证明局部PAV最优蕴含价格性。同时构造了一种将分配公理提升到份额划分的构造方法，并利用该移植关系发现多个在委员会选举中不成立但可近似成立的蕴含关系。

Result: 证明了核心比例性公理（EJR、EJR+、FJR）的等价性，以及次核心公理（PJR、PJR+、FPJR）的等价性；建立了Lindahl价格性与PAV得分近似最优性的等价；证明局部PAV最优的委员会是价格性的；给出了从分配公理到份额划分的提升构造，并揭示了多个在委员会选举中不成立但可近似成立的新蕴含关系。

Conclusion: 审批制分配在公理层面比委员会选举更接近份额划分，该模型是研究委员会选举中近似比例性关系的理想平台；多个公理等价性与蕴含关系的发现为后续在委员会选举中建立近似公理提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study approval-based apportionment, a variant of committee elections in which candidates ("parties") can be selected several times. We show that the proportionality axioms EJR, EJR+, and FJR coincide, and so do PJR, PJR+, and FPJR; that Lindahl priceability, an axiom implying core stability, is equivalent to a notion of approximate optimality with respect to the proportional approval voting (PAV) score; and that locally PAV-optimal committees are priceable.
  Approval-based apportionment (where a candidate receives an integer number of seats) lies between committee elections (zero or one seat) and portioning (a fractional number of seats). We formally connect portioning and apportionment by giving a construction that lifts axioms from apportionment to portioning and preserves implications between them. Several of our new implications between apportionment axioms are natural from a portioning perspective, leading us to believe that apportionment sits closer to portioning than to committee elections. None of them holds in committee elections, but several extend approximately, which makes apportionment a fruitful setting for conjecturing approximate relationships in approval-based committee elections.

</details>


### [317] [Condorcet-Winning Sets and Peer Selection in Planar Metric Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27653)
*Gabriel de Azevedo,Ulysse Hennebelle*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究平面度量选举中的Condorcet获胜集，提出了一个统一的二人Stackelberg博弈框架，将一般度量选举、同伴选择和一轮Voronoi博弈纳入同一层级。主要成果包括：在ℓ1和ℓ∞范数下证明任意平面实例存在大小至多3的Condorcet获胜集（即使对强同伴选择也成立），这基于新的强矩形ε-网结果；在ℓ2范数下证明大小至多4的Condorcet获胜集存在，改进此前5的界；并为Voronoi博弈给出新的范数无关界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Condorcet获胜集是排名选择投票中的一个核心概念，在现实生活中如委员会选举、同行评审等领域有广泛应用。此前研究者对平面度量选举中Condorcet获胜集的大小仅给出了较宽松的上界（如一般5的界），且不同问题（一般度量选举、同伴选择、Voronoi博弈）缺乏统一的框架。本文旨在统一这些问题并改进已知的界。

Method: 本文将一般度量选举、同伴选择和一轮Voronoi博弈统一建模为一个二人Stackelberg博弈，并置于共同的问题层级中，引入强同伴选择这一新变体。技术核心是证明一个新的强矩形ε-网结果：对平面上任意点集，可选取至多三个输入点，使得每个包含超过一半点的轴平行矩形都能被击中，从而改进Ashok等人的9/16阈值。基于此结果导出ℓ1和ℓ∞范数下的Condorcet获胜集大小至多3；在ℓ2范数下通过几何论证将上界从5改进到4；对Voronoi博弈给出范数无关的新界。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) ℓ1和ℓ∞范数下，任意平面实例存在大小至多3的Condorcet获胜集，即使对强同伴选择也成立；(2) 强矩形ε-网的选取阈值从9/16改进到能够实现至多3个点覆盖所有含超过一半点的轴平行矩形；(3) ℓ2范数下，平面度量选举的Condorcet获胜集大小至多4，改进此前Song等人的5的上界；(4) 为一轮Voronoi博弈给出新的范数无关界。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一博弈框架揭示了多类投票/Voronoi问题之间的结构联系，并在多个范数下显著改进了Condorcet获胜集大小的上界，特别是ℓ1/ℓ∞下的最优常数3以及ℓ2下的4。这些结果不仅深化了对平面度量选举的理论理解，也为同伴选择等实际场景提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In ranked-choice voting, a Condorcet-winning set is a group of candidates for which no outside candidate is preferred to every member of the group by a majority of voters. We study Condorcet-winning sets in planar metric elections, where voters rank candidates according to their distance under a given norm. We formulate general metric elections, peer selection, and the one-round Voronoi game as instances of a two-player Stackelberg game and place these problems in a common hierarchy. We also introduce a new variant, which we call strong peer selection.
  Our main result concerns peer selection under the $\ell_1$ and $\ell_\infty$ norms. We prove that every planar instance admits a Condorcet-winning set of size at most three, even under strong peer selection. This follows from a new result for strong rectangular $\varepsilon$-nets. We show that, for every set of points in the plane, one can choose at most three input points that intersect every axis-parallel rectangle containing more than half of the points, improving the previous threshold of $9 / 16$ due to Ashok et al. Under the $\ell_2$ norm, we prove that every planar metric election admits a Condorcet-winning set of size at most four, improving on the general bound of five due to Song et al. Finally, we give new norm-independent bounds for the one-round Voronoi game.

</details>


### [318] [Horizon-Independent Contraction for Continuous-Time Discounted Regularized Mean-Field Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27723)
*Junji Yan,Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了折扣与熵正则化下非平稳连续时间平均场博弈（MFG）的收缩性质。证明了在足够大的折扣率下，有限时域MFG存在与时间域无关的收缩条件，且该条件与无限时域非平稳收缩条件一致，并由此得到有限与无限时域均衡之间的显式收敛速率。此外，通过一个正算子的谱半径推导了逐时间域的精细收缩准则，并证明了无限时域极限与收缩因子一致，最后给出了折扣与非折扣有限时域正则化均衡之间的显式误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于MFG收缩性质的研究多集中于无折扣情形或平稳问题，对带折扣的有限时域非平稳MFG缺乏系统的收缩性分析；有限时域与无限时域均衡之间的关系也尚未在折扣与熵正则化框架下得到清晰刻画。

Method: 以受控连续时间马尔可夫链刻画代表性智能体状态，采用折扣与熵正则化框架；通过构造与时间域无关的收缩条件（在足够大的折扣率下成立），利用正算子谱半径刻画策略误差传播并推导精细收缩准则，进而建立有限与无限时域均衡的收敛速率及折扣与非折扣均衡的误差界。

Result: （1）足够大折扣率下，有限时域MFG存在时间域无关的收缩条件，与无限时域非平稳收缩条件一致；（2）得到有限与无限时域平均场均衡之间的显式收敛速率；（3）从正算子谱半径导出逐有限时域的精细收缩准则，其大时域极限与收缩因子一致；（4）给出折扣与非折扣有限时域正则化均衡之间的显式误差界。

Conclusion: 该研究为带折扣和熵正则化的非平稳连续时间MFG提供了系统的收缩性理论，建立了有限与无限时域均衡之间的定量联系，并为均衡计算与误差分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study contraction properties of non-stationary continuous-time mean-field games (MFGs) under discounting and entropy regularization. The state of the representative agent evolves according to a controlled continuous-time Markov chain, and both the state and action spaces are finite. In contrast to the undiscounted case, we show that, under a sufficiently large discount rate, finite-horizon MFGs admit a horizon-independent contraction condition, which also coincides with the corresponding infinite-horizon non-stationary contraction condition. As a byproduct, we obtain an explicit convergence rate between finite- and infinite-horizon mean-field equilibria. For each finite horizon, we further derive a refined contraction criterion from the spectral radius of a positive operator that majorizes the propagation of policy errors, and show that its large-horizon limit agrees with the horizon-independent contraction factor. Finally, we provide an explicit error bound between discounted and undiscounted finite-horizon regularized equilibria.

</details>


### [319] [Fine-Tuning Autobidders with Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28199)
*Anton Safin,Alexandra Khirianova,Andrey Pudovikov,Aleksandr Katrutsa,Egor Samosvat*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出将GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）框架从大语言模型后训练领域迁移到自动出价（autobidding）场景，作为一种无评论家（critic-free）的策略梯度方法，解决传统actor-critic强化学习在自动出价中训练不稳定、对噪声鲁棒性差的问题。在BAT、iPinYou和AuctionNet基准上，该方法在点击量指标上持续优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动出价是现代在线广告系统的核心组件，常被建模为马尔可夫决策过程并用actor-critic强化学习训练出价生成函数。然而，actor-critic中actor网络和critic网络的交替训练会导致训练不稳定且对噪声鲁棒性下降，且自动出价场景中最优出价未知，缺乏ground-truth标签，这与大语言模型后训练中的奖励未知问题具有相似性。

Method: 将GRPO框架适配到自动出价场景。GRPO是无评论家的策略梯度方法，无需critic网络估计价值，避免了actor-critic交替训练的不稳定性。该方法借鉴LLM后训练中用GRPO微调预训练模型的思想，将GRPO用于增强强启发式基线的性能。模型以出价或相关的调速乘数为动作，以期望拍卖回报为价值目标。

Result: 在BAT、iPinYou和AuctionNet三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，Autobidding GRPO在点击量指标上持续优于actor-critic模型、简单启发式方法和基于控制器的方法，在转化量指标上达到最好或次好的性能。

Conclusion: GRPO框架可以有效迁移到自动出价领域，作为一种critic-free的策略梯度方法，既解决了actor-critic训练不稳定的问题，又能利用GRPO在LLM领域的技巧（如基于组内相对奖励的优势估计）来增强启发式基线的性能，为自动出价提供了一种稳健且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated bidding (autobidding) is a core component of modern online advertising systems. Within this component, advertisers delegate sequential bid decisions to algorithms that must maximize campaign value while adhering to constraints such as a limited budget and a target cost-per-click (CPC). One of the approaches to resolve the autobidding problem is to formulate it as a Markov decision process and use reinforcement learning (RL) to train a bid generation function. The standard RL framework is actor-critic, which consists of an actor network that generates actions and a critic network that estimates the value of those actions. In our setting, the action is typically a bid or related pacing multiplier, and the value is the expected return from the auction given the bid. However, the alternating training of actor-critic RL models leads to instability and reduced robustness to noise. To address these issues, we adapt the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework to the autobidding setting. This framework is a \emph{critic-free} policy-gradient method originally developed for large language model post-training, where the ground-truth target is unknown. The autobidding setting shares this property, since the optimal bid is unknown in advance. Moreover, GRPO in the LLM domain is used to fine-tune the pre-trained model, and we use the same technique to enhance the performance of the strong heuristic baseline. We empirically compare Autobidding GRPO with actor-critic models, simple heuristics, and controller-based methods on the BAT, iPinYou, and AuctionNet benchmarks. Extensive experiments show that Autobidding GRPO consistently outperforms baselines in clicks and is the best or second-best method in conversion volume.

</details>


### [320] [A Constant Metric Distortion Protocol for Approval Voting Given Plurality Polls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28340)
*Fabian Frank,Jannik Peters*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文首次研究在仅有"多数学民意调查"（即每个候选人作为第一选择所获选民比例）这一简单信息下如何实施批准投票（approval voting），提出一个由参数k∈(0,1)控制的可批准最小区间协议，并证明了在最优k下该规则的度量失真（metric distortion）达到2+√5≈4.236。同时证明Bucklin投票规则的度量失真上界为5，并验证了协议对噪声的鲁棒性以及上界的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批准投票中选民的"批准集"难以确定：如果选民对候选人存在真实的严格偏好序，那么他们应该批准哪些候选人是模糊的。现有研究通常假设完全排名信息或复杂的启发式方法，但现实中往往只能获得部分信息。作者希望利用最简单的"多数学民意调查"（即每个候选人获得的第一名票数占比）为选民提供清晰的批准策略，从而弥合理论投票规则与实际情况之间的差距。

Method: 作者提出一个简单协议：每个选民批准其偏好列表中包含至少k比例选民第一选择的"最小区间"（即前缀）。该协议仅依赖多数学民意调查信息。作者使用度量失真框架（metric distortion）进行评估，其核心思想是假设选民和候选人位于同一度量空间中，目标是选择使总距离最小化的候选人。作者通过优化参数k求得最优度量失真，并利用相关证明技巧推导Bucklin规则的上界，最后通过构造对抗性实例（adversarial instances）证明上界是紧的，并检验协议对噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 所提协议在最优参数选择下达到度量失真2+√5≈4.236；(2) Bucklin投票规则的度量失真上界为5；(3) 协议对噪声具有鲁棒性；(4) 所得上界是紧的（tight）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅凭极简单的多数学民意调查信息，批准投票就能实现良好的度量失真保证（4.236），远好于通用上界。同时该协议简单易实施，为实践中的批准投票提供了清晰且有针对性的选民行为指引。Bucklin规则5的失真上界也是新结果。未来可进一步研究更复杂信息条件下的改进以及该协议的其他性质。

Abstract: Approval voting is a simple and well-regarded voting rule: voters submit approval ballots (subsets of the candidates) and the candidate receiving the most approvals wins. One major limitation of approval voting is that it is not clear which candidates voters should approve if they have an underlying strict order over the candidates. In this paper, we initiate the study of approval voting under a simple kind of information: plurality polls. That is, we assume that for each candidate we know the share of voters who rank this candidate as their top choice. Using these plurality polls, we suggest a simple protocol parameterized by a fraction $k \in (0,1)$: every voter should approve the smallest prefix of their preference list containing the first choices of at least a fraction $k$ of the voters. We evaluate this protocol via the framework of metric distortion and show that for the optimal choice of $k$, this rule achieves a metric distortion of $2 + \sqrt{5} \simeq 4.236$. The proof techniques we use for this statement also show that the Bucklin voting rule has a metric distortion of at most $5$. Finally, we evaluate the robustness of our protocol to noise and show that the upper bounds obtained for our protocol are tight.

</details>
